### [1] [Note on proliferation transitions of non-Abelian anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20604)
*Meng Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了由任意子增殖驱动的（2+1）维拓扑相变，通过将TQFT与子范畴中的任意子耦合，利用SymTFT框架将动力学局域在对称边界上，并给出显式的Chern-Simons-Higgs场论实现，包括非可逆一形式对称性规范化的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑相中任意子增殖驱动的相变是一个重要而尚未完全解决的理论问题，特别是涉及非可逆一形式对称性的规范化时，缺乏统一的框架和显式的场论实现。

Method: 作者提出一个通用理论框架：将拓扑量子场论（TQFT）与辫子融合子范畴中的任意子对应的动力学物质耦合，并利用对称拓扑场论（SymTFT）解释该构造，使动力学局域在（3+1）维板的对称边界上。随后通过若干具体示例给出显式的Chern-Simons-Higgs（CSH）场论实现，包括Rep(G)一形式对称性的一般构造和SU(2)_10→Spin(5)_1的非可逆一形式规范变换。

Result: 论文给出了任意子增殖相变的通用理论框架和SymTFT解释，提供了多个CSH场论实现示例，并特别构造了实现非可逆一形式对称性规范化的CSH理论，讨论了这些相变中具体增殖的任意子类型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个系统的理论框架来描述任意子增殖驱动的拓扑相变，并将其与非可逆一形式对称性的规范化联系起来，为未来的拓扑相变研究和材料实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study phase transitions out of a (2+1)d topological phase driven by the proliferation of anyons in a braided fusion subcategory. We describe a general theoretical framework for such transitions, in which the topological quantum field theory (TQFT) is coupled to dynamical matter associated with anyons in the subcategory. We interpret this construction using symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT), where the dynamics is localized on the symmetry boundary of the (3+1)d slab. We analyze several examples, and present explicit Chern-Simons-Higgs (CSH) field theories realizing the proliferation transitions. We also examine proliferation transitions that implement gauging of non-invertible one-form symmetry in the parent TQFT. This includes a general construction for ${\rm Rep}(G)$ one-form symmetry for a finite group $G$, and a CSH realization of the non-invertible one-form gauging ${\rm SU}(2)_{10}\rightarrow {\rm Spin}(5)_1$. We discuss which anyons proliferate at these transitions.

</details>


### [2] [Distinguishing Mott and strain-induced anisotropic semimetals in magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene using Landau level spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20694)
*Keshav Singh,Juan Felipe Mendez-Valderrama,Erez Berg,B. Andrei Bernevig,Oskar Vafek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于量子扭转显微镜实验，提出用面外磁场下朗道能级带隙大小层级作为判据，区分魔角扭转双层石墨烯在电荷中性的两种候选态：莫特半金属和应变诱导各向异性半金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角扭转双层石墨烯的量子扭转显微镜实验揭示了窄带中重电子与轻电子特征的鲜明二元性，但符合光谱特征的两种候选态（强动态关联的莫特半金属与弱哈特里-福克效应的应变诱导各向异性半金属）难以区分，亟需一个清晰可靠的判别准则。

Method: 作者聚焦于电荷中性点，提出利用面外磁场作为判别探针，比较两种状态下预期朗道能级带隙大小的层级结构。对每个Chern数C的带隙Δ_C进行系统分析，并检验了应变取向和晶格弛豫对结果鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 应变诱导的各向异性半金属具有稳健的带隙层级：Δ_{\pm 4} > Δ_{\pm 8} > Δ_{\pm 12}；而莫特半金属中Δ_{\pm 4}始终最大，但C = ±8、±12的带隙相对大小敏感依赖于外加磁场。应变半金属的带隙层级与有限磁场下的不可压缩性测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 外磁场下的朗道能级带隙层级可作为区分莫特半金属与应变诱导各向异性半金属的清晰判据，且应变半金属的预测与已有实验测量吻合，支持其作为与实验光谱特征一致的候选态。

Abstract: Recent quantum twisting microscopy experiments [1] have provided unprecedented momentum- and energy-resolved imaging of magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene, revealing a stark dichotomy between the heavy and light electronic characters of its narrow bands. In this work, we provide a sharp distinguishing criterion between the two most likely candidate states that exhibit spectroscopic features consistent with the experimental results. Focusing on charge neutrality, these states are 1) the 'Mott semimetal', governed by strong dynamical correlations, and 2) the 'strain-induced anisotropic semimetal', characterized by weak Hartree-Fock effects. We demonstrate that an out-of- plane magnetic field serves as a sharp discriminating probe based on the expected Landau level gap size hierarchy. We find that the strain-induced anisotropic semimetal has a robust gap hierarchy where the Chern C gaps, Δ_C , decrease in order Δ_{\pm 4} > Δ_{\pm 8} > Δ_{\pm 12}. In contrast, for the Mott semimetal, Δ_{\pm 4} remains the largest gap, while the relative magnitude of the C = \pm 8, \pm 12 gaps depends sensitively on the applied magnetic field. We test the robustness of these results against strain orientation and lattice relaxation finding consistency between the gap hierarchy of the strained semimetal and previous incompressibility measurements at finite magnetic field [2].

</details>


### [3] [Electronic Toroidal Metals: Landau Theory and Magnetoelectric Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20838)
*Yuhang Xiao,Ning Hao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个电子环形金属（ETM）的通用理论框架，证明了环形序（一种高阶多极序）可以从巡游电子系统中自发涌现，表现为费米液体不稳定性，并建立了环形矩与反对称磁电响应、赝自旋纹理拓扑以及非线性输运之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环形序是一种高阶多极序，但其在巡游电子系统中的内在实现此前尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立一个通用的理论框架来理解和描述由巡游电子自由度自发产生的环形金属。

Method: 作者首先提出了投影不稳定性判据，以去除不能凝聚的均匀电流分量（因为候选环形双线性算符在对称性上可能与均匀电荷电流重叠）。在电流正交的扇形中，他们在P奇且T奇的粒子-空穴通道中识别出软集体模式，从而证明ETM作为费米液体不稳定性出现。随后通过反对称磁电响应张量定义巡游系统中的环形矩，并建立该响应与赝自旋纹理拓扑之间的联系，同时分析ETM的非线性电荷输运特性。

Result: 结果表明：(1) ETM作为费米液体不稳定性自发涌现；(2) 环形矩可通过反对称磁电响应张量精确定义；(3) 赝自旋涡旋的重排会改变费米面的绕数结构并显著增强磁电响应；(4) ETM表现出特征性的非线性电荷输运，包括带间量子几何贡献的显著增强。

Conclusion: 环形电子金属是一种独特的金属朗道相，该工作为理解巡游电子产生的环形序提供了通用框架，确立了ETM作为新的物质量子相的地位。

Abstract: Toroidal order is a higher-rank multipolar order whose intrinsic realization in itinerant-electron systems remains unexplored. Here, we develop a generic theory of the electronic toroidal metal (ETM), in which toroidal order emerges spontaneously from electronic degrees of freedom near the Fermi surfaces. Because candidate toroidal bilinears can overlap by symmetry with the uniform charge current, we formulate a projected instability criterion that removes the noncondensable current component. Within this current-orthogonal sector, we identify a soft collective mode in the $\mathcal{P}$-odd and $\mathcal{T}$-odd particle-hole channel and demonstrate that ETM arises as a Fermi-liquid instability. We then define the toroidal moment in an itinerant system through the antisymmetric magnetoelectric response tensor. We further establish an intimate connection between this response and the topology of pseudospin texture: the rearrangement of pseudospin vortices changes the winding structure of the Fermi surfaces and markedly enhances the magnetoelectric response. ETM also exhibits characteristic nonlinear charge transport, including a pronounced enhancement of its interband quantum-geometric contribution. Our results establish ETM as a distinct metallic Landau phase and provide a general framework for understanding toroidal order generated by itinerant electrons.

</details>


### [4] [Doping-driven Mott transition from steady-state density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21029)
*David Jacob,Stefan Kurth*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文在稳态密度泛函理论（i-DFT）框架下描述了Hubbard模型中由掺杂驱动的Mott转变。作者利用费米液体理论推导了i-DFT的交换关联（xc）偏压与多体理论中准粒子权重之间的联系，并以此指导构建近似泛函。数值结果表明该泛函确实能够捕捉掺杂驱动的Mott转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在i-DFT框架下描述Hubbard模型中的掺杂驱动Mott转变，而实现这一目标的关键在于获得任意密度和电流下交换关联偏压的近似，因此需要一种系统的构建该近似的方法。

Method: 采用费米液体理论建立i-DFT的交换关联偏压与多体理论准粒子权重之间的解析联系，基于此关系指导构建xc偏压的近似泛函，并在Hubbard模型中通过数值计算验证该泛函的适用性。

Result: 所构建的近似泛函能够成功捕捉掺杂驱动的Mott转变，表明i-DFT可以作为描述这一强关联现象的可行理论框架。

Conclusion: i-DFT在适当的xc偏压近似下能够描述掺杂驱动的Mott转变，且费米液体理论为构建此类近似提供了有效的理论指导。

Abstract: The doping-driven Mott transition in the Hubbard model is described within the frame- work of steady-state density functional theory, or i-DFT. In order to access the many-body spectral function in i-DFT, an approximation for the exchange-correlation (xc) bias at arbitrary density and current is required. By using Fermi-liquid theory, a connection between the xc bias of i-DFT and the quasiparticle weight of many-body theory is derived and the results are employed to guide the construction of the approximation. Numerical results obtained with this functional demonstrate that the doping-driven Mott transition can indeed be captured with i-DFT.

</details>


### [5] [Berry-Curvature Effect in Anomalous Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21161)
*V. P. Mineev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于准经典矩阵动力学方程的替代推导方法，用于描述交变磁体和非中心对称金属中由Berry相位效应引起的反常输运现象，包括反常霍尔效应、反常热流和反常热电电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Di Xiao等人基于场依赖态密度建立了Berry相位效应在反常输运中的理论框架，但作者希望提供一种替代性的推导路径，以更自然地描述交变磁体和非中心对称金属中的Berry相位效应，并统一推导反常霍尔、反常热流和反常热电等输运性质。

Method: 采用准经典矩阵动力学方程（quasiclassic matrix kinetic equation）的框架，替代原有基于场依赖态密度的推导方法，系统地推导Berry相位效应在反常输运中的贡献。

Result: 成功推导出了交变磁体和非中心对称金属中的反常霍尔效应、反常热流以及反常热电电流。

Conclusion: 准经典矩阵动力学方程为Berry相位效应在反常输运中的描述提供了一种新的、统一的推导途径，可推广到交变磁体和非中心对称金属体系。

Abstract: A theory of the Berry-phase effect in anomalous transport in ferromagnets driven by statistical forces such as the gradient of temperature or chemical potential has been developed by Di Xiao et al in Phys. Rev. Lett. 97, 026603 (2006) based on a field-dependent density of states generating an additional contribution to the current density. We present an alternative derivation of the Berry- phase effect in anomalous transport in altermagnets and noncentrosymmertric metals based on quasiclassic matrix kinetic equation. Along with anomalous Hall effect an anomalous heat flow and anomalous thermalelectric currents are also derived.

</details>


### [6] [Emergent Vibronic Spectral Hierarchy in a Kagome Flat-Band Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21250)
*Jun Shu,Jun Shen,Yanmin Zhang,Hong Du,Qingsong Wang,Zeyuan Wang,Bin Wang,Zeliang Xu,Dengjing Wang,Hengfu Lin,Zunming Lu,Lei Qin,Jie Yuan,Jinbo Peng,Zhida Song,Fedor V Kusmartsev,Anna Kusmartseva,Kui Jin,Ruidan Zhong,Ge He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了kagome化合物Rb₂Ni₃S₄在T*≈260-280 K附近的非常规绝缘相变，发现该相变涉及电子局域化但无常规结构或磁有序参数。通过偏振分辨拉曼光谱等技术，观测到与近无色散333.7 cm⁻¹声子相关的巨Franck-Condon级联，叠加40.6 cm⁻¹间距的梳状精细结构，两者嵌套共存被解释为强耦合电子-振动流形（含局域化晶格坐标）的证据，表明无色散声子是固体内振动子（vibronic）关联的活跃途径，与绝缘相密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子耦合通常在色散晶格振动的准粒子框架下被理解，而局域化或弱色散声子模式所主导的互补极限所知甚少。本文旨在探索这一未充分研究的领域，特别是在kagome化合物中无色散声子如何驱动电子-晶格耦合及绝缘相变。

Method: 综合运用偏振分辨拉曼光谱、变温X射线衍射、扫描隧道显微镜以及电学、热学和磁性测量手段，系统表征Rb₂Ni₃S₄在相变前后的结构、电子和振动性质，并分析拉曼光谱中的Franck-Condon级联和梳状精细结构的磁场响应。

Result: 相变不伴随常规结构或磁有序参数；拉曼光谱显示近无色散333.7 cm⁻¹声子产生巨Franck-Condon级联，叠加40.6 cm⁻¹等间距梳状结构；梳间距对磁场不敏感，但其谱权重受磁场强烈调制。

Conclusion: 嵌套共存的Franck-Condon级联与梳状结构表明存在强耦合的电子-振动流形，涉及局域化晶格坐标。无色散声子提供了一种电子-振动自陷（vibronic self-trapping）的活跃途径，该机制与Rb₂Ni₃S₄的绝缘相密切相关。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling is usually understood in terms of electronic quasiparticles interacting with dispersive lattice vibrations. Much less is known about the complementary limit in which the relevant phonon mode is itself localized or weakly dispersive. Here we investigate this regime in the kagome compound Rb$_{2}$Ni$_{3}$S$_{4}$, which undergoes an unconventional insulating transition near $T^{*} \approx$ 260-280~K. Combining polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy with temperature-dependent x-ray diffraction, scanning tunneling microscopy, and electrical, thermal, and magnetic measurements, we show that the transition involves electronic localization without a conventional structural or magnetic order parameter. Raman spectra reveal a giant Franck-Condon progression associated with a nearly dispersionless 333.7~cm$^{-1}$ phonon, decorated by an equally spaced comb-like fine structure with a characteristic spacing of 40.6~cm$^{-1}$. The comb spacing is insensitive to magnetic field, whereas its spectral weight is strongly field tunable. Rather than treating either hierarchy alone as pure phonon effect, we interpret their nested coexistence as evidence for a strongly coupled electron-vibrational manifold involving a localized lattice coordinate. These results identify dispersionless phonons as an active route to vibronic correlations in solids and suggest that such electron-vibrational self-trapping is closely associated with the insulating phase of Rb$_{2}$Ni$_{3}$S$_{4}$.

</details>

*Dongwook Kim,Karsten Held*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了铜氧化物超导体中红外（MIR）光学电导率的普适特征来源于两个不同的能隙：由反铁磁自旋涨落介导的赝能隙和Mott-Hubbard能隙。这两个能隙在MIR光谱中呈现特征性且可区分的形状和掺杂依赖性，数值与解析计算的掺杂依赖性均与实验一致，提供了对铜氧化物MIR光学电导率的统一微观理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体的中红外光学电导率展现出普适特征，但其微观起源尚不明确。理解这些特征背后的物理机制对于揭示高温超导的微观机理至关重要。

Method: 通过数值与解析方法相结合，研究MIR光学电导率中两个能隙（赝能隙和Mott-Hubbard能隙）的贡献。分析了随时间掺杂变化时Mott-Hubbard关联如何从可见光区峰值转变为MIR宽光学响应，并与一粒子谱中出现的"waterfall"结构相关联。

Result: 两个能隙在MIR光谱中表现出不同且特征性的形状和掺杂依赖性，可被区分。数值和解析确定的两个能隙的掺杂依赖性均与实验观测一致，成功复现实验数据。

Conclusion: 该研究为铜氧化物MIR光学电导率提供了统一的微观理解，表明其普适特征源于赝能隙与Mott-Hubbard能隙的共同贡献，而非单一机制。

Abstract: Mid-infrared (MIR) optical conductivities in cuprate superconductors show universal features. In this study, we demonstrate that these originate from two distinct gaps: the pseudogap mediated by antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations and the Mott-Hubbard gap. The MIR spectra from these two gaps show a characteristic and distinct shape and doping dependence, and can thus be distinguished. Specifically, the Mott-Hubbard correlations, while rendering a peak in the visible spectrum for small dopings, yield a broad optical response starting in the MIR when a ``waterfall'' develops in the one-particle spectrum at larger dopings. The numerically and analytically determined doping-dependence of both gaps consistently reproduce experiments, and provide a unified microscopic understanding of the cuprate MIR optical conductivity.

</details>


### [8] [Neural quantum states in condensed matter: advances, best practices, and prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21291)
*Jonas B. Rigo,Björn J. Wurst,Rajah Nutakki,Markus Schmitt,Dante Kennes*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文回顾了神经量子态在凝聚态物理中的应用进展，涵盖受挫量子磁体、相互作用格点费米子及非平衡动力学，讨论了架构设计、对称性约束、优化方法和采样策略，并总结了可靠模拟的实用指南及当前主要挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子多体系统的经典模拟面临指数级计算复杂度的根本困难，神经量子态通过将神经网络参数化与蒙特卡洛采样相结合，为量子多体波函数提供了一种灵活的变分表示，有望拓展经典模拟的边界。

Method: 以综述视角系统梳理神经量子态的近期进展，从架构设计、对称性约束、优化方法、采样策略等多个技术维度展开讨论，并总结面向可靠模拟的实践指南与开放挑战。

Result: 综述了神经量子态在受挫量子磁性、相互作用格点费米子和非平衡动力学等三方面的代表性成果，识别出学习非平凡符号/相位结构、控制变分偏差、实施物理对称性、扩展大规模网络优化及实现稳定实时演化等关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 神经量子态虽已展现潜力，仍面临多项根本性挑战，但通过克服这些障碍，有望显著拓展对强关联量子物质的经典模拟能力。

Abstract: Neural quantum states provide flexible variational representations of quantum many-body wave functions by combining neural-network parametrizations with Monte Carlo sampling. In this perspective, we review recent advances in their application to condensed-matter systems, focusing on frustrated quantum magnets, interacting lattice fermions, and non-equilibrium dynamics. We discuss the architectures, symmetry constraints, optimization methods, and sampling strategies underlying state-of-the-art calculations, and summarize practical guidelines for reliable simulations. We also examine the principal remaining challenges, including learning non-trivial sign and phase structures, controlling variational bias, enforcing physical symmetries, scaling optimization to large networks, and achieving stable real-time evolution. Finally, we outline promising directions in which neural quantum states may extend the reach of classical simulations of strongly correlated quantum matter.

</details>


### [9] [Ultralow-Field Triplon Condensation in a Spin-Ladder Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21316)

Thermodynamic, magnetoelastic, $μ$SR, and neutron-diffraction measurements overturn the previous assignment of zero-field antiferromagnetic order, establishing a quantum-disordered coupled-ladder parent state with persistent low-energy dynamics. The weak low-temperature anomaly instead marks a gap-controlled crossover from the correlated ladder regime into the activated quantum-disordered state. These measurements further reveal an exceptionally asymmetric ordered dome extending to $μ_0 H_{c2}\simeq 8.2$ T. Quantum Monte Carlo simulations for the relevant spin Hamiltonian place Henmilite just on the gapped side of the zero-field ladder-ordering instability, naturally accounting for the strong separation between the exchange and residual-gap scales and the tiny critical field. Our findings extend ultralow-field triplon condensation beyond the dimer paradigm and establish Henmilite as a platform for controlled tuning across quantum criticality in a fluctuation-dominated spin ladder.

</details>

### [10] [Strong growth and Goldstein subgradients in piecewise smooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20642)
*Adrian S. Lewis,Fahaar M. Pirani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种针对Lipschitz函数在局部极小值点附近的新的增长条件，该条件来源于Goldstein次梯度算法的实际行为研究。对于分段二次连续可微的目标函数，证明了在强凸条件下该增长条件成立；在非凸情形下，证明了二次增长结合活动梯度的正则性性质也足以保证该条件成立，并通过计算实验验证了假设的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldstein次梯度算法在实际应用中经常观测到近似线性收敛行为，但理论上对其收敛速率的理解尚不充分。本文旨在探究一个新的增长条件，该条件能解释这些算法在实践中观测到的良好收敛性能，并明确在何种函数类别下该条件成立。

Method: 针对分段二次连续可微（piecewise C^2）的目标函数类，从两个角度建立新增长条件成立的充分条件：（1）强凸情形下的直接证明；（2）非凸情形下，证明二次增长（quadratic growth）加上活动梯度正则性（regularity property for active gradients）即可保证新增长条件成立。并通过数值实验验证所提假设的必要性和充分性。

Result: 在分段二次连续可微函数的框架下，证明了：当目标函数强凸时，新增长条件成立；在非凸情形下，二次增长与活动梯度的正则性性质联合足以保证新增长条件成立。计算实验进一步表明这些假设条件在实践中是重要的且不可随意放宽。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解Goldstein次梯度算法在实践中的近似线性收敛行为提供了理论支撑，明确了新增长条件在分段光滑函数类中的成立条件，即强凸性或二次增长加活动梯度正则性，从而深化了对非光滑非凸优化算法收敛性质的认识。

Abstract: We explore a new growth condition for Lipschitz functions around local minimizers, recently proposed in the context of Goldstein-subgradient-based algorithms and their behavior in practice. On generic examples, these algorithms are often observed to converge approximately linearly. We focus on objectives that are piecewise twice continuously differentiable. In that case, the new growth condition holds when the objective is, in addition, strongly convex. In the nonconvex case, we prove that quadratic growth in conjunction with a regularity property for the active gradients suffices. Computational experiments illustrate the importance of the assumptions.

</details>

*Junxiang Xu,Vinayak Dixit,S. Travis Waller,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Qianwen,Guo,Sisi Jian,Xiao Wen,Ashutosh Ashutosh,Sunhyung Yoo,Julius Secadiningrat,Jingni Guo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ResiliFlow是一个开放的交通世界模型平台，将灾害交通韧性分析（六项地图中心功能）与AI交通基础设施感知决策支持（街道级/卫星证据、八类可见状况识别）整合到统一的八步循环工作流中，支持从观察、验证到可审查决策的完整链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通韧性研究分散在数据预处理脚本、网络模型、仿真工具、图像检测系统和报告中，导致从观测到可测试、可审查决策的转化困难，缺乏一体化、可复用且可审查的分析平台。

Method: 构建ResiliFlow平台，连接两个工作区：1）灾害交通韧性分析工作区，提供关键道路/区域识别、恢复优先级排序、中断绕行、韧性测试和情景仿真等六项地图中心功能；2）AI交通基础设施感知与决策支持工作区，整合街道级与卫星证据，通过计算机视觉检测道路、人行道和路缘的可见状况。两个工作区共享八步循环（感知→预测→模型开发→验证→执行→决策→反馈→记忆），并通过研究验证模块记录假设与检查，本地助手与可选多提供商大语言模型助手将用户问题转化为受限的可执行工具调用。

Result: 论文展示了平台架构、代表性数学模型、界面证据和计算机视觉学习结果。实验在八个可见状况类别上实现了准确识别，紧凑的错误分析展示了困难案例如何引导持续学习。

Conclusion: ResiliFlow证明交通模型可以成为可检查、可复用、问题驱动的系统，而非孤立分析的集合。其发布版本旨在支持研究协作、公共审查和机构监督下的扩展。

Abstract: Transport resilience work is often split across separate data preparation scripts, network models, simulation tools, image inspection systems and reports. This fragmentation makes it difficult to move from an observation to a tested and reviewable decision. We introduce ResiliFlow, an open transport world model concept and an implemented platform for infrastructure resilience, response and recovery. The platform connects two workspaces. Disaster Transport Resilience Analysis provides six map-centred functions for critical-road and critical-area identification, recovery prioritisation, disruption routing, resilience testing and scenario simulation. AI-based Transport Infrastructure Perception and Decision Support organises street-level and satellite evidence, detects visible road, footpath and kerb conditions, and prepares these observations for human-reviewed intervention planning. Both workspaces share an eight-step cycle of perception, prediction, model development, verification, execution, decision, feedback and memory. Research Validation records assumptions and checks, while a local Assistant and an optional multi-provider large language model Copilot translate user questions into bounded calls to executable tools. We document the platform architecture, representative mathematical models, interface evidence and computer-vision learning results. Examples show accurate recognition across eight visible-condition classes, while compact error analysis demonstrates how difficult cases guide continued learning. ResiliFlow shows how transport models can become an inspectable, reusable and question-led system rather than a collection of disconnected analyses. The accompanying release is intended to support research collaboration, public scrutiny and extension under institutional review.

</details>


### [12] [Improving Stability and Economic Operation in Transmission Systems through Volt/VAR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20747)
*Shuaicheng Tong,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同伦的连续化方法，用于解决输电系统中考虑离散控制设备（有载调压变压器和电容器组）的电压/无功优化（VVO）问题，在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上的实验表明，该方法能在实际运行时间内产生满足交流可行性的设定点，同时降低电压偏差、无功调度量和发电成本，并可与现有输电调度实践自然集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输电系统运营商通常通过交流潮流（ACPF）求解来调和市场化清算的直流调度与交流物理特性，但所得设定点仍可能违反电压（Volt）和无功（VAR）限制。因此需要能够在运行中快速实施的纠正性决策，以维持安全的电压分布和充分的VAR支撑。现有方法在离散控制设备（如OLTC和电容器组）的协调方面存在不足。

Method: 提出一种基于同伦（homotopy）的连续化方法，用于离散控制变量的电压/无功优化（VVO），该方法协调开关类设备（有载调压变压器OLTC和电容器组CB）。实验在IEEE、PEGASE和RTE系统上进行验证。

Result: 所提出的VVO方法能在实际运行时间内产生交流可行的设定点，同时降低电压偏差、VAR调度量和发电成本。此外，VVO在使用ACPF调整后的直流调度作为输入时，与使用交流可行调度作为输入时性能相当，表明该方法可与现有输电调度实践自然集成。

Conclusion: 该同伦连续化VVO方法能够有效解决离散控制下的电压/无功优化问题，在保证AC可行性的前提下兼顾计算效率和运行性能，并与现有基于DC调度的输电运行流程兼容，可加强电网运行的安全性与经济性。

Abstract: Transmission system operators often reconcile market-cleared DC dispatches with AC physics through power-flow solves (ACPF), yet the resulting setpoints can still violate voltage (Volt) and reactive-power (VAR) limits. Maintaining secure voltage profiles and adequate VAR support therefore requires fast corrective decisions that are implementable in operation. This paper presents a novel homotopy-based continuation method for discrete-control Volt/VAR Optimization (VVO) that coordinates switchable devices, such as on-load tap-changing transformers (OLTCs) and capacitor banks (CBs). Experiments on IEEE, PEGASE, and RTE systems show that the proposed VVO produces AC-feasible setpoints within practical runtime, while reducing voltage deviation, VAR dispatch, and generation cost. VVO also achieves comparable performance using ACPF-adjusted DC dispatches as inputs relative to AC-feasible dispatches, indicating that it can be integrated naturally into existing transmission dispatch practices to strengthen grid operation.

</details>


### [13] [Time-Inconsistent MDPs with Entropy Regularization: Equilibrium Existence and Policy Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20811)
*Fengyuan Cao,Zhenhua Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究无限时域、具有可数无穷状态空间和无界奖励函数的时间不一致马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），奖励显式依赖初始时间和状态。通过熵正则化和加权泛函分析方法，证明了正则化松弛均衡的存在性，并利用紧性、Gibbs策略集中性和一致可积性论证获得原非正则化问题的松弛均衡。此外，还研究了策略迭代算法（PIA），在加权折扣结构下建立了指数收敛性和正则化均衡的唯一性，并给出了迭代策略构成原问题加权ε-均衡的显式遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致性问题在动态决策中广泛存在（如个体偏好随时间变化），传统动态规划方法不再适用。已有研究多局限于有界奖励或可数状态空间的受限情形，本文旨在处理更一般的场景：无限时域、可数无穷状态空间、无界且显式依赖初始时间和状态的奖励函数，并建立正则化松弛均衡的存在性理论及可计算的策略迭代算法。

Method: 采用熵正则化技术，将原始问题转化为正则化问题，通过固定点算子刻画正则化松弛均衡。引入两个功能不同的权函数——一个控制奖励和值函数的增长，另一个定义环境加权空间——在乘积拓扑下构造紧不变集，并利用Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理证明正则化均衡的存在性，且不变集可对λ∈(0,1]统一选取。随后令λ→0+，通过紧性、Gibbs策略浓度和一致可积性论证得到原问题的松弛均衡。对PIA，在加权折扣结构下利用加权Banach空间建立指数收敛性和唯一性，并结合软最大化近似界推导加权ε-均衡的遗憾估计。

Result: 证明了正则化松弛均衡的存在性，并通过极限论证获得了原非正则化问题的松弛均衡存在性。对策略迭代算法，在充分强折扣条件下建立了指数收敛性和唯一性，给出了迭代策略作为加权ε-均衡的显式遗憾界。数值算例验证了强折扣下PIA的收敛性，反例则展示了弱折扣下PIA可能失败。

Conclusion: 本文为广义时间不一致MDP（包括无界奖励和可数无穷状态空间）提供了系统的理论框架，熵正则化结合加权泛函分析的方法既能保证均衡的存在性，又能通过PIA实现高效求解。强折扣条件下算法的指数收敛性和遗憾界保证了方法的实际可计算性，而弱折扣反例揭示了算法的适用边界。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon time-inconsistent Markov decision processes with a countably infinite state space and unbounded reward functions. The reward is allowed to depend explicitly on the initial time and initial state, thereby accommodating general sources of time inconsistency. We seek relaxed feedback equilibria, and our approach is based on entropy regularization and weighted functional analytic methods. With entropy regularization, we characterize a regular relaxed equilibrium through a fixed-point operator. By introducing two weight functions with distinct roles, one controlling the growth of rewards and values and the other defining the ambient weighted space, we construct a compact invariant set under a product topology and apply the Schauder-Tychonoff fixed-point theorem to establish existence of regularized equilibria. Importantly, the invariant set can be chosen uniformly for small entropy weight $λ\in(0,1]$. We then let $λ\to0+$ and show, through compactness, concentration of Gibbs policies, and uniform-integrability arguments, that a subsequential limit is a relaxed equilibrium of the original unregularized problem.
  We further study a policy iteration algorithm (PIA) for the entropy-regularized equilibrium problem. Under a weighted-discounting structure and sufficiently strong discounting, we establish exponential convergence and uniqueness of the regularized equilibrium in a suitable weighted Banach space. Combining the policy-iteration error with a quantitative soft-max approximation bound, we show that the iterated policies constitute weighted $\varepsilon$-equilibria for the original unregularized problem and derive an explicit regret estimate. A numerical example illustrating the convergence of PIA under strong discounting and a counterexample demonstrating its failure under weak discounting are also provided.

</details>


### [14] [Failure of Whole-Sequence Convergence in Variable-Smoothing Full-Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20859)
*Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Boţ、Li和Tao在SIAM J. Optim.上提出的结构化非凸非光滑分式规划的平滑全分裂近端次梯度方法(S-FSPS)，证伪了"全序列收敛"的猜想。作者构造了两个符合条件的实例，其原始序列的聚类集为{1}×S¹且轨迹无限长，尽管每个聚类点都是精确极限提升平稳点。这是首个明确构造表明：即使满足精确极限平稳性，非可和消失平滑也不蕴含全分裂方案的全体序列收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛理论仅证明了S-FSPS方法存在子序列收敛到精确极限提升平稳点，但整个生成序列是否必须收敛的问题悬而未决。回答这一问题对理解该算法的完整收敛行为至关重要，也对变平滑全分裂类方案的理论基础有深远影响。

Method: 作者通过构造反例来否定全序列收敛性质。具体构造了两个符合条件的实例：第一个构造中线性算子A非零且每个生成的对偶迭代非零；第二个构造中可行集是全维的、A具有满行秩，且f∘K与g∘A在可行集上均非常数。两个构造的原始序列聚类集均为{1}×S¹并具有无限长轨迹。此外，第一个构造还推广到对应变平滑单循环全分裂方法，产生了非收敛的例子。

Result: 作者明确回答了"整个生成序列是否必须收敛"这一问题，答案为否。两个构造的实例证明了序列可以每个聚类点都是精确极限提升平稳点，但整个序列不收敛。第一个构造还产生了变平滑单循环全分裂方法在非凸非光滑复合优化中的非收敛例子。这是首个此类显式构造。

Conclusion: 文章证明了S-FSPS方法在现有假设条件下不具备全序列收敛性质，指出了该方法的收敛理论边界。该结果对变平滑全分裂方案具有一般性启示：即使平滑参数非可和地趋于零且所有聚类点是精确极限平稳点，也不能保证整个序列的收敛性。这为未来研究指出了需要更强假设或改进算法设计的必要性。

Abstract: We investigate whole-sequence convergence of the smoothing-based full-splitting proximal subgradient method (S-FSPS) for structured nonconvex and nonsmooth fractional programs, introduced as Algorithm~4.1 by Boţ, Li, and Tao (\emph{SIAM J. Optim.}, 35(4):2623--2653, 2025). The existing convergence theory establishes only the existence of subsequences converging to exact limiting lifted stationary points, leaving open whether the entire generated sequence must converge. We answer this question in the negative by constructing two admissible instances whose primal sequences have cluster set \(\{1\}\times\mathbb S^1\) and infinite-length trajectories, although every cluster point is an exact limiting lifted stationary point. In the first construction, the linear operator \(A\) is nonzero and every generated dual iterate is nonzero. In the second, the feasible set is full-dimensional, \(A\) has full row rank, and both \(f\circ K\) and \(g\circ A\) are nonconstant on the feasible set. The first construction also yields a nonconvergent example for the corresponding variable-smoothing, single-loop, full-splitting method for nonconvex and nonsmooth composite optimization, although a subsequence converges to an exact limiting stationary point. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction showing that vanishing nonsummable smoothing, even together with exact limiting stationarity, does not imply whole-sequence convergence for variable-smoothing full-splitting schemes.

</details>


### [15] [Maximum Covering Network Design on Graphs with Low Connectivity: Dynamic Programming and Block-Cut Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20894)
*Felix Rauh,Jannik Matuschke,Hande Yaman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究最大覆盖网络设计问题（MCNDP），在共享预算下同时决策设施选址与网络升级，提出一种基于低连通网络（树与割点）的精确动态规划框架，可在单次运行中获得完整覆盖-预算曲线，并在超过80%的测试用例中优于直接求解MILP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划公共服务设施（如医疗、应急响应、学校）时，不仅要选择设施位置，还需改善连接人们与设施的网络（如升级易受灾道路）。然而现有选址模型通常将网络视为固定、预算视为给定。此外，该问题即便在最简单的网络上也难以求解，且规划者需要看到覆盖随预算增长的完整曲线，而非单一方案。

Method: 提出一个精确动态规划框架，利用真实道路网络低连通性的特性（存在大量割点）。在树上，递归自包含，每个状态化简为少量快速计算的设施与升级选择，无需求解器，运行时间可预测；在一般低连通网络上，框架在割点处分解问题，嵌入给定的MILP公式求解各个子问题，通过界面覆盖条件协调各片段。所有预算水平在单次运行中同时评估，可获得完整覆盖-预算曲线。

Result: 在306个测试用例中，框架与直接求解相比，在超过80%的实例上达到或优于直接MILP求解的性能；对于较大预算和旅行距离，比直接求解MILP公式更快；单次运行即可获得全部预算水平下的覆盖-预算曲线，而直接求解需将时间分摊到各预算上。

Conclusion: 该动态规划框架利用低连通网络的结构特性，在无需额外成本的情况下提供完整的覆盖-预算权衡曲线，为最大覆盖网络设计问题提供了高效且可扩展的精确求解方案。

Abstract: Planning accessible public services such as health care, emergency response, and schools often requires not only choosing where to open facilities but also improving the network that connects people to them, for example upgrading flood-prone roads in vulnerable regions. Most location models, however, take the network as fixed and the budget as given. We study the Maximum Covering Network Design Problem, in which a single budget is shared between opening facilities and upgrading weak links to maximize the population within a target travel distance of an open facility. The problem is hard even on the simplest networks, and planners usually want to see how coverage grows with the budget, not a single plan.
  We develop an exact dynamic-programming framework that exploits a property common to real road networks: their low connectivity, with many cut points whose removal disconnects the network. On trees, the recursion is self-contained: each state reduces to a few simple facility and upgrade choices that are fast to compute without a solver, giving predictable running times; for larger budgets and travel distances it outperforms solving the MILP formulation directly. On general low-connectivity networks, the framework decomposes the problem at the cut points and embeds a given MILP formulation to solve the resulting pieces, coordinating them through coverage conditions at the interfaces. This lets us compare a formulation on its own against the same formulation inside the framework: across 306 test cases the framework matches or outperforms direct solving on more than 80% of instances. Because it evaluates all budget levels in a single run, it also yields the full coverage-versus-budget curve at no extra cost, whereas direct solving must split its time across individual budgets.

</details>


### [16] [Bridging Semantics and Behavior: An Agent-Based Evolutionary Model of Topic Competition with BERTopic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20996)
*Blekanov I.,Gubar E.,Fan X*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语言无关的计算框架，将BERTopic语义主题建模与演化博弈论及NetLogo智能体仿真相结合，在微博数据上建模信息传播与观点演化，并通过微观模仿与噪声容忍机制复现宏观话题扩散趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中的信息传播与观点演化受社会学习、感知效用和认知限制共同驱动，但传统模型往往将内容与行为割裂，未能捕捉个体认知与社会互动如何联合驱动大规模话语的形成与发展。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：使用BERTopic从大规模微博数据中提取连贯主题；基于点赞、评论和转发构建统一的参与度量化指标；在NetLogo中构建智能体仿真，智能体通过局部模仿更新主题偏好，并依据收益比较和行为噪声（即有限理性）调整策略。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的测试表明，该模型成功复现了观测到的话题扩散趋势，并揭示了社会学习频率与收益敏感性如何塑造公众注意力，证明了微观层面的模仿与噪声容忍机制可涌现出宏观层面的传播模式。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将语义分析与行为仿真链接，为人机交互领域提供了关于在线集体行为认知与社会基础的新见解，并在错误信息韧性、观点预测和自适应内容策展方面具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Understanding how information spreads and opinions evolve on social media is a core challenge in computational social science. Users constantly face competing topics, yet their decisions to engage are shaped by social learning, perceived utility, and cognitive limits. Traditional models often separate content from behavior, missing how individual cognition and social interaction jointly drive large-scale discourse.
  We propose a language-independent computational framework that bridges semantic topic modeling with evolutionary game theory and agent-based simulation. Using BERTopic, we extract coherent topics from a large Weibo dataset. We then quantify engagement through a unified metric based on likes, comments, and reposts. In NetLogo, agents update topic preferences via local imitation, guided by payoff comparisons and behavioral noise---mirroring real-world bounded rationality.
  Tests on two real-world datasets show that our model successfully replicates observed topic diffusion trends. It highlights how social learning frequency and payoff sensitivity shape public attention. Our results demonstrate that micro-level mechanisms---imitation and noise-tolerant choice---can produce emergent macro-level patterns. This work contributes to human-computer interaction by linking semantic analysis with behavioral simulation, offering insights into the cognitive and social foundations of online collective behavior. The framework also has practical potential for misinformation resilience, opinion forecasting, and adaptive content curation.

</details>

*R. P. P. F. Goetz,Y. Dwaraga,N. van de Wouw,T. Oomen,M. M. J. van de Wal,B. Sharif,H. J. Zwart*

TL;DR: 本文针对三维热弹性系统中卡尔曼滤波估计性能最大化的传感器布置问题（NP-hard组合优化问题），提出了一种结合基于Gramian预滤波器的改进二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法，有效降低了计算复杂度并实现了快速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器布置问题本身是NP-hard的组合优化问题，其可行解空间随候选位置和传感器数量呈组合级增长，直接求解计算代价极高，需要高效的启发式算法。

Method: 将三维热弹性系统建模为离散时间线性随机模型，采用引入Gramian预滤波器的改进二进制人工蜂群（NBABC）算法来搜索最优传感器布置方案，利用Gramian矩阵相关的可观测性度量在搜索前过滤低质量候选，以减少计算复杂度。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在求解效率上表现优异，收敛速度快，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: NBABC算法结合Gramian预过滤是解决三维热弹性系统传感器布置问题的有效途径，能够在可接受的计算成本内获得高质量的传感器配置方案。

Abstract: Sensor placement for maximizing the estimation performance of the Kalman filter is an NP-hard optimization problem. Furthermore, its feasible set grows combinatorially with the candidate locations and the number of sensors. In this paper, we study this sensor placement problem for a 3D thermoelastic system modelled as a discrete-time linear stochastic model. We use the Novel Binary Artificial Bee Colony (NBABC) algorithm with a Gramian-based pre-filter to reduce the computational complexity. Our results show the efficiency and the fast convergence of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [18] [Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS): Integrating Established Prioritization Operators for Priority Elicitation in the Analytic Hierarchy Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21187)
*Kevin Kam Fung Yuen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对层次分析法（AHP）中直接最小二乘（DLS）方法因非凸性导致多个全局最小值、优先级排序不稳定的问题，提出了锚定正则化直接最小二乘（ARDLS）优化模型。该模型通过将归一化技术、特征向量法、奇异值分解、余弦最大化及伪逆Gram矩阵等既有优先级算子作为理论锚点引入正则化惩罚，打破数学对称性，保证收敛到唯一全局最小值。数值实验验证了ARDLS在降低RMSE的同时保证严格数学唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AHP中DLS方法虽直观且无需复杂变换，但因其非凸性在高度不一致性（如循环矛盾）下会产生多个不同的全局最小值，导致优先级排序不稳定且严重依赖初始猜测值，这是该方法的结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出ARDLS优化模型，将多种已确立的优先级算子（归一化、特征向量法、SVD、余弦最大化、伪逆Gram矩阵/加权最小二乘闭式解）作为理论锚点嵌入正则化惩罚项，通过系统性地打破数学对称性来倾斜优化景观，从而保证收敛到唯一全局最小值。

Result: 全面的数值实验与模拟验证表明，ARDLS框架在保证严格数学唯一性的同时，成功降低了各既有优先级算子之间的均方根误差（RMSE）。

Conclusion: ARDLS有望成为AHP在多应用领域中的理想替代方案，克服了DLS非凸性导致的解不稳定问题。

Abstract: Pairwise reciprocal matrices are fundamental to the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP), a decision-making model. While the Direct Least Squares (DLS) method provides an intuitive mechanism for deriving priority vectors without complex transformations, the DLS provides multiple solutions. Under high levels of inconsistency, such as cyclic contradictions, this non-convexity yields multiple distinct global minima, resulting in unstable priority rankings that critically depend on initial algorithmic guesses. To overcome this structural deficiency, this paper introduces the Anchored Regularized Direct Least Squares (ARDLS) optimization model. ARDLS integrates uniquely determined established prioritization operators, such as normalization techniques, the Eigenvector method, Singular Value Decomposition, Cosine Maximization, and the Pseudo-Inverse Gram Matrix (the closed-form solution of Weighted Least Squares), as theoretical anchors within a regularization penalty. This integration systematically breaks mathematical symmetries, tilting the optimization landscape to guarantee convergence upon a single, unique global minimum. Comprehensive numerical experiments and simulations validate that the ARDLS framework successfully reduces root mean square error among established priority operators, while guaranteeing strict mathematical uniqueness. The proposed ARDLS may be the ideal alternative for the AHP applied to many application domains.

</details>


### [19] [Primal Acceleration of Newton's Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21359)
*Nikita Doikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的直接加速牛顿法，用于最小化具有Lipschitz连续Hessian的凸函数。该算法仅使用原始变量，每次迭代只需一次线性求解，以简单预定的参数选择实现了O(1/k^3)的全局收敛率，并可通过Bregman散度扩展到任意几何及复合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二阶优化方法中，虽然三次正则化等方法可以达到O(1/k^3)的加速收敛率，但它们通常需要求解昂贵的非线性正则化子问题或进行非线性参数搜索。现有方法缺乏一种既保持快速全局收敛率又仅依赖单次线性系统求解的简单直接算法。

Method: 该算法是一种直接加速的牛顿方法，采用预先确定的简单参数选择，每次迭代仅执行一次线性系统求解，仅使用原始变量（不使用对偶变量进行额外梯度修正）。该方法可通过非精确线性求解器以Hessian-free方式实现，并通过Bregman散度推广到任意几何结构，同时扩展到复合优化问题。

Result: 算法实现了关于函数残差的全局收敛率O(1/k^3)，这是该问题类别中首个仅依赖每次迭代一次线性系统求解即可达到此速率的方法（无需求解如三次正则化等辅助非线性正则化子问题、执行非线性参数搜索或使用对偶外梯度修正）。非精确求解器实现仍保持快速全局收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了加速二阶方法的一个重要空白：通过极简的算法结构（仅原始变量、一次线性求解、预定参数）即可达到最优的全局收敛率，同时方法具备灵活的扩展性（Hessian-free实现、Bregman几何、复合优化），具有理论优雅性与实际可用性的双重优势。

Abstract: We develop a new direct accelerated Newton method for minimizing convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessian. The algorithm uses only primal variables and performs just one linear solve per iteration. With a simple predetermined choice of parameters, it achieves the global convergence rate of $O(1/k^3)$ in terms of the functional residual. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first second-order method for this problem class attaining this rate while relying solely on one linear system solve per iteration (without solving auxiliary nonlinear regularized subproblems, such as cubic regularization, performing nonlinear parameter searches, or using dual extragradient corrections). Our method can be implemented in a Hessian-free way, using an inexact linear system solver, while preserving the fast global rate. We further extend our construction to arbitrary geometry through Bregman divergence, and to composite optimization problems.

</details>

### [20] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将音乐类人机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的实验接口，批判当前依赖预录音频刺激的研究范式无法捕捉真实音乐体验的多模态、具身和交互特性，并通过WAS-5机器人案例论证其技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音乐情感研究受限于基于预录音频刺激的实验范式，无法反映真实音乐体验中多模态、具身和交互的本质，需要新的方法论平台来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 基于音乐认知与情感理论，识别需要同时控制声学与非声学变量的机制；论证类人机器人具备参数化控制、跨试验可复现性以及声学/视觉/交互组件解耦与重组的能力；通过WAS-5机器人案例展示其可实现的可控性。

Result: 通过WAS-5案例研究证明了音乐类人机器人在可控性方面的技术可行性，包括声学与交互变量的可复现控制，满足未来音乐情感实验的前置条件。

Conclusion: 音乐类人机器人可作为方法论平台，支持未来对音乐诱发情绪进行受控的、可重复的实验研究，弥补传统范式的不足。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [21] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一种用于双向内镜控制的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过显式语言指令条件化与隐变量条件化整流流动作专家相结合，解决内镜控制中的"意图别名化"（intent aliasing）歧义问题。实验表明其导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向推进减少83%，闭环成功率达30百分点提升，并在离体猪气管实验中10/10通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠道内镜操作本质上是双向的：器械需要前进到达目标解剖位置，随后后撤或反转进行检查，同时外部指令可能要求提前反向。当请求的操作阶段在视觉场景变化之前发生改变时，几乎相同的视觉观察可能需要相反的轴向动作。这种双向内镜控制中的歧义被识别并形式化为"意图别名化"（intent aliasing）问题，现有方法未能显式处理此歧义。

Method: EndoLIFT采用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略架构，结合两种条件化机制：(1) 显式语言指令条件化，用于选择轴向操作模式；(2) 32维变分轨迹隐变量（VTL）随机条件化连续动作块（action-chunk）生成。策略输入为RGB图像、语言指令和上一动作状态；同时设计了架构受控的1位模式标志参考模型用于消融对比。

Result: 相比无隐变量条件化的匹配模型，EndoLIFT导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向推进减少83%。1位模式标志参考模型表现出较弱的锚点切换能力，而EndoLIFT在44个未见语言变体上保持82.8%的意图跟随准确率。闭环评估中，EndoLIFT在结肠模体以及未见肺和胃模体上相比无VTL版本提升30个百分点成功率，并在离体猪气管实验中10/10完成。

Conclusion: 实验结果将语言驱动的意图选择与轨迹隐变量对方向正确性和稳健回撤的贡献进行了分离验证，证明语言条件化负责轴向模式选择，而轨迹隐变量独立地增强方向的正确性和拉回的鲁棒性，两者协同构成完整的双向内镜控制方案。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [22] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同设计框架，同时开发数据采集设备与机器人执行设备。基于该框架设计了Koala Gripper系统——一个集数据采集与机器人抓取于一体的平台，通过创新的力优化手指/扳机联动机构、单片式双拇指结构和人体工学设计，在保持可扩展性和易用性的同时，显著提升了灵巧性和抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模操作数据集的日益增长，手持式机器人抓取器数据采集及其对应设计变得愈发重要。然而现有数据采集设备设计趋向于匹配现有机器人抓取器的形态，牺牲了人体工效学和操作性能。因此需要一种能同时考虑数据采集和机器人执行两个环节约束的设计框架。

Method: 提出协同设计(co-design)框架，将数据采集设备和机器人执行设备的平台约束同时纳入设计流程。基于该框架设计了Koala Gripper，包括：1) 创新的力优化手指/扳机联动机构，具有定向反射质量特性；2) 独特的单片式双拇指结构；3) 以用户为中心的人体工学设计。执行手指具有反向驱动性，有效质量在数十克量级。

Result: 该抓取器在广泛的物体范围内实现稳定抓取，支持强力工具使用和精确分离操作。通过端到端的数据采集与策略执行流水线部署验证了平台能力，展示了从示范学习的效果。

Conclusion: Koala Gripper系统证明了协同设计方法可以有效平衡数据采集端的人体工效学与执行端的抓取性能，在保留可扩展性和易用性的同时提升了灵巧性和抓取能力，为机器人操作数据采集设备的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [23] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Logic-VLA是一个形式需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过信号时序逻辑（STL）规范编码器和两阶段策略适配（STL条件监督微调+轨迹级偏好优化），使单一VLA能适应不同STL形式要求，同时保持自然语言任务性能。在四旋翼导航仿真中，相比STL盲基线策略，STL满足率提升24.8-40.7个百分点，而NL任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型可以跟随自然语言任务指令，但自然语言指令无法精确表达安全关键或时空相关的要求。这可能导致模型行为无法满足形式化的硬性约束，引发安全隐患或任务失败。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：1）基于语法图的STL编码器，通过预训练捕获时序逻辑语义；2）两阶段策略适配：首先进行STL条件监督微调（用满足演示数据），然后利用流匹配替代进行Identity Preference Optimization的轨迹级偏好优化（用匹配的满足-违反轨迹对）；3）推理时以STL规范作为条件输入。

Result: 在封闭环路四旋翼导航仿真中，跨随机化照片逼真环境评估并对未见过STL公式测试泛化能力，Logic-VLA相比STL盲基线策略将STL满足率提升24.8至40.7个百分点，自然语言任务成功率最多仅降低1.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一VLA能够在不依赖每种规格单独策略的情况下，动态适应不同的形式化STL要求，同时保持自然语言任务的成功率几乎不受影响，证明了将形式逻辑条件融入VLA框架的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [24] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）相结合，用于固定翼无人机的规划与控制。同时引入风感知采样策略，在强风环境下生成动态可行的参考轨迹，并通过仿真与真实飞行实验验证了跟踪精度和鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机由于非线性动力学、气动限制和环境扰动，其规划与控制具有挑战性。微分平坦性虽能高效生成可行轨迹，但主要局限于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需要大量参数调节。

Method: 提出统一框架，将微分平坦性轨迹生成与NMPC集成，兼顾计算效率与预测、约束感知能力；并在NMPC框架内引入风感知采样策略，生成考虑风扰动且严格满足气动和控制输入约束的动态可行参考轨迹。

Result: 通过广泛仿真和真实飞行实验验证，该框架在复杂轨迹上提升了跟踪精度和鲁棒性，特别是在强风条件下采用风感知采样策略时效果显著。

Conclusion: 融合微分平坦性和NMPC的统一框架能够有效提升固定翼无人机在强风等扰动环境下的轨迹跟踪性能，验证了风感知采样策略的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [25] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"重训练校准审计"框架，用于评估机器人模仿学习中的示范数据"遗忘"效果。该框架沿"行为"和"证据"两个轴衡量编辑后策略是否等效于从数据中移除相关示范后重训练的策略，并通过一致化检验结合两轴对联合重训练一致性进行假设检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖人类示范数据，用户可能事后要求删除其中某些示范。重训练（移除数据后再训练）是自然参照，但成本随策略与数据集规模增长而增加，因此需要更廉价的算子对已训练策略进行编辑。现有机器学习遗忘评测指标（如遗忘损失或单一成员推理攻击）无法确立闭环策略中一次编辑到底移除了什么。

Method: 提出"重训练校准审计"，沿两轴评估示范遗忘：(1) 行为轴——测量编辑策略与移除示范后重训练策略在匹配状态下的动作散度，以独立重训练间的散度作为地板校准，达到地板即视为与重训练足够接近；(2) 证据轴——对每条示范施加针对重训练零分布的成员推理攻击，同时报告其排名与绝对成员损失水平，因仅凭排名会接受那些将成员损失膨胀至零分布之上的算子。随后用一致化检验将两轴合并为联合重训练一致性的单一假设，对照足够多的独立重训练舰队在常规显著性水平上做出拒绝判定。

Result: 在三类真实机器人策略与两个仿真套件上预设了五个条件，结果显示两个轴在某个检查点上可双向分离：编辑可能修复任务行为却保留证据不变，也可能减少证据却使行为偏离重训练。在ACT（Action Chunking Transformer）架构上，重定向编辑将盲评的机器人任务成功率恢复到18/20次试验。

Conclusion: 所提出的重训练校准审计能为编辑型策略赋予闭环机器人行为中的遗忘效果提供更可信的度量，并揭示行为与证据两轴的分离现象，说明仅凭单一轴评估不足以判定编辑是否真正等效于重训练。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [26] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需演示或参考轨迹、从零开始通过强化学习合成类人坐站（STS）动作的方法。单策略PPO在三个互补组件（耦合力/椅高课程、逆运动学生成的多样化初始与目标姿态采样、受生物力学和最优控制启发的奖励设计）驱动下，在八个椅子高度上达到超过97%的平衡站立成功率，并能从比现有方法更深的坐姿站起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从坐姿站起（STS）是一个极具挑战性的控制问题，因为存在无数种在保持平衡的前提下起身的方式。现有方法依赖示范或参考轨迹，泛化能力受限。如何从零开始合成自然、平滑且鲁棒的STS动作，并避免过早分布偏移导致泛化崩溃，是核心动机。

Method: 采用单一PPO策略从零学习STS。方法包含三大组件：(i) 耦合的力/椅高课程：垂直骨盆辅助力帮助早期轨迹探索并随时间衰减，更高的椅子在辅助力衰减后才解锁，确保策略在每个椅高掌握可行轨迹后再面对更难的情况；(ii) 通过逆运动学生成覆盖八个椅子高度的大量初始与目标姿态并随机采样，增强动作鲁棒性；(iii) 受生物力学与最优控制启发的奖励设计，塑造坐离阶段的角动量，并通过压力中心吸引函数支持支撑区域转换，实现平滑低能耗驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，策略在八个椅子高度上的平衡站立成功率超过97%。策略在不同椅高间产生平滑泛化动作，并能从比现有最先进方法明显更深的坐姿站起。

Conclusion: 通过课程学习、多样化姿态采样和生物力学启发奖励的协同作用，无需任何示范即可从零合成自然、鲁棒且可泛化的类人STS动作，显著优于现有方法在深坐姿场景下的表现。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [27] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形软地面上完成起立动作。利用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模地形柔顺性，结合参考动作追踪与显式恢复奖励，先在硬地面训练再降低地面刚度实现策略迁移，最终成功在软硬地面完成倒地到站立的恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在软地面上起立是一个具有挑战性的任务，因为地面变形导致支撑力延迟产生，传统基于硬地面录制的演示策略无法直接迁移。现有方法缺乏对地形柔顺性的显式建模，需要在仿真中模拟可变形地面并实现从硬到软地面的策略自适应。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：(1) 使用参考动作追踪（残差关节位置控制）和显式恢复奖励（骨盆高度、躯干直立度、末态姿势）在硬地面上训练策略；(2) 通过更新solref降低地面刚度并扩展solimp标称表面穿透区域，使策略适应接触密集阶段的延迟支撑力。奖励由参考追踪奖励和恢复目标的奖励共同构成。

Result: 学习到的策略在仿真中成功完成倒地到站立任务，达到目标骨盆高度和直立度，过程中最大接触穿透约40mm。方法在两组起立序列上得到验证，在硬软地面上均成功实现最终恢复目标。消融实验表明仅依赖参考追踪无法完成起立，显式恢复奖励至关重要。

Conclusion: 参考引导强化学习结合显式恢复奖励是解决软地面人形机器人起立任务的有效途径；通过MuJoCo软接触模型参数调节可实现从硬到软地面的策略迁移，在保持原始演示模式的同时适应地面变形带来的动力学变化。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [28] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向X构型四旋翼的无惯性传感器视觉状态估计系统，仅依赖双目相机图像和电机推力指令，在机体坐标系中使用连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）估计姿态、速度、角速度、重力及扰动等状态，并通过4视图光束平差生成带速度信息的稀疏场景流点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼状态估计严重依赖GPS和IMU等传感器，在GPS拒止环境或传感器失效场景下难以工作。本文旨在构建一种纯视觉、无IMU、无GPS、完全在机体坐标系内运行的状态估计系统，利用双目相机和推力指令实现自包含的定位与运动感知，为下游世界模型提供测量来源。

Method: 系统采用以下技术路线：（1）连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器作用于复合流形状态空间SE(3)×R³×...，估计机体坐标系下的位姿、速度、角速度、重力和扰动；（2）特征检测使用FAST/Shi-Tomasi，时间跟踪使用SSD/Lucas-Kanade，跨相机匹配使用NCC，搜索区域由滤波器导出的位姿与点不确定性预测；（3）卡方门控（Chi-squared gating）基于归一化新息筛选静态特征点作为隐式惯性参考；（4）4视图（两个时间戳的双目对）全光束平差联合估计特征点的位置与速度，利用双目视差和时间视差，以滤波器导出的相对位姿作为先验；（5）点云密度自适应分配，由外部焦点引导在感兴趣区域生成稠密覆盖。

Result: 系统输出机体坐标系状态估计、标定后的位姿变化以及稀疏场景流。点云中的每个点携带位置、速度和联合协方差信息，且点云密度根据外部焦点自适应分配。EKF中的特征点不直接进入求解器，其信息通过位姿先验间接反映。

Conclusion: 该系统完全独立于GPS、IMU和世界坐标系基础设施，可在无惯性测量的条件下提供可靠的机体坐标系状态估计和场景流输出，架构上预留了未来集成GPS和IMU的扩展空间，适用于作为当前机体坐标系锚定的下游世界模型的测量源。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [29] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的、DevOps驱动的虚拟场景测试框架，利用轻量级Kubernetes（K3s）集群、Helm/Helmfile、Argo Workflows等容器编排工具，实现CARLA中自动驾驶系统场景测试的自动化构建、部署和分布式并行执行。在200个场景的多节点集群评估中，最优配置相比串行基线将端到端工作流加速超过8倍，并已在真实ADS测试应用中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统（ADS）和智能交通系统（ITS）的验证依赖大规模虚拟场景测试，但包含数千个场景的测试套件执行过程劳动密集且难以扩展。现有方法缺乏自动化、可端到端管理的分布式测试工作流，亟需一个能自动打包、部署并并行执行大规模仿真测试的框架。

Method: 构建了一个基于DevOps理念的端到端框架：1) 将ROS 2应用打包为标准化的Kubernetes Helm图表；2) 通过动态Helmfile清单声明式编排完整仿真环境；3) 采用Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境配置、OpenSCENARIO测试用例的聚合与批处理、跨集群节点并行执行、日志和资源指标收集；4) 在轻量级多节点K3s集群上部署CARLA模拟器进行分布式测试。

Result: 在多节点K3s集群上运行200个场景的评估中，最优配置使端到端工作流时间相比串行基线加速超过8倍。结果量化了并行度、编排开销和集群稳定性之间的权衡，并在真实ADS测试应用中验证了框架可行性，包括场景源连接和下游评估模块集成。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为可扩展的仿真测试提供了坚实基础，还能生成可追溯的证据链以支持安全论证，为大规模ADS虚拟测试提供了一条切实可行的自动化路径。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>

*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种快速双臂运动规划管线，采用领导者-跟随者参数化方法，将刚性物体操作中双臂末端执行器之间的相对变换约束作为硬约束在整个路径上持续满足，在模拟中比先前工作快19.4倍，并在真实Kinova Gen3双臂平台上验证了轨迹可直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作在执行复杂任务时涉及大量自由度，处理刚性物体时两个末端执行器之间的相对变换必须在整个运动过程中保持固定，这构成一个非线性等式约束，将可行构型空间限制在零测度流形上，给传统运动规划器带来极大挑战。

Method: 采用领导者-跟随者参数化：将领导者的构型视为自由变量，跟随者的构型通过逆运动学求解以严格满足刚性变换约束。该硬约束在整个路径上连续施加，而非仅在离散采样点处近似满足。

Result: 在多种仿真环境、不同约束条件和双臂平台上进行评估，规划速度比先前工作快19.4倍，同时保证约束的连续满足。在双臂Kinova Gen3平台上进行的托盘搬运和细长物体操作真实实验验证了规划轨迹可直接迁移至物理硬件。

Conclusion: 该方法通过领导者-跟随者参数化有效解决了双臂刚性物体操作中的非线性等式约束难题，在显著提升规划速度的同时保证全程约束满足，且具备良好的仿真到实物迁移能力。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [31] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模仿学习的端到端局部规划器，通过轻量级离线基元数据集收集框架生成安全高质量的轨迹基元，紧凑神经网络将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数，实现亚毫秒级实时轨迹生成，无需后端求解，计算快（桌面端<1ms，机载平均3.68ms）、内存占用低（<1.5MiB），并在仿真与真实实验中验证了鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知杂乱环境中的自主飞行受限于机载轨迹生成在计算量、轨迹质量与内存占用三方面的权衡困境（computation-quality-memory trilemma），传统方法难以同时兼顾实时性与高质量轨迹生成。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级离线基元数据收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全高质量的轨迹基元；2) 采用模仿学习训练紧凑神经网络，将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数（其内在编码高阶动力学信息）；3) 学习到的策略无需后端求解即可实时生成平滑、经验上无碰撞且动力学可行的轨迹。

Result: 计算极快（标准台式机低于1ms，机载飞行平均3.68ms），内存占用低（小于1.5MiB）；仿真基准测试在规划延迟和目标到达进度质量上均优于对比方法；零样本真实世界实验验证了鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该端到端学习方法有效解决了自主飞行中计算-质量-内存的三难困境，在不牺牲轨迹质量的前提下实现了超快规划和极低内存开销，并通过真实的零样本部署验证了方法的实际可用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [32] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合辊式-颗粒阻塞（roller-jamming）抓取器，将主动物体吸入与抓取后保持功能集成于单一夹爪中。该抓取器通过内旋辊增加接触并将物体拉向中心，随后利用真空诱导的颗粒阻塞使辊变硬以稳定抓取。在840次抓取试验中实现了812次成功（96.7%的成功率），消融实验表明辊式与颗粒阻塞机制各自贡献不同，验证了混合策略在改善非理想首次接触后的物体获取与保持方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在家庭场景的物体操纵中，位姿不确定性常导致偏离中心或部分接触的初始抓取，使得可靠获取物体变得困难。现有的辊式抓取器虽能主动吸入物体但捕获后稳定性有限，而颗粒阻塞抓取器则需要充分接触才能实现强保持。因此需要一种兼具主动吸入和抓取后保持能力的新型抓取器。

Method: 提出混合辊-阻塞抓取器：通过内旋辊旋转增加接触并将物体拉向夹爪中心，随后施加真空诱导颗粒阻塞使辊硬化并稳定抓取。同时给出了简化的几何分析及样机的力能力台架表征。样机安装于7自由度机械臂上，使用8个测试物体，在受控平面位置偏移和方位角偏移条件下进行系统评估，包括840次主评估试验和162次消融试验（对比辊-only与阻塞-only条件）。

Result: 主评估中，抓取器在840次试验中成功812次：平面偏移试验215/216次成功，方位角偏移试验597/624次成功。消融实验中，辊-only和阻塞-only条件分别在81次试验中成功54次和24次，表明两种机制各自贡献不同，混合策略优于任一单独机制。

Conclusion: 实验结果为混合辊-阻塞策略提供了初步的机制层面证据，表明该方法在非理想首次接触后改善物体获取与保持方面具有前景，是一种有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [33] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCaP是一种物理信息驱动的代码即策略（Code-as-Policy）智能体，用于机器人操作中的主动感知。它通过物理信息探索层实现显式信息寻求，利用无需训练的物理属性提取模块从机器人本体感觉中估计物体质量与刚度，并采用双智能体设计（Planner决定何时探索/停止，Prioritizer过滤并排序交互）来平衡探索成本与信息效率。在真实世界桌面操作任务和LIBERO模拟任务中，PhysCaP在更少交互和更短执行时间下达到与基线相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然在模仿演示方面表现出色，但依赖被动观察，无法推断对操作至关重要的潜在物理属性（如质量、刚度等）。这限制了其在物理属性隐含或被遮挡场景中的应用，因此需要一种能够主动、有目的地与环境交互以获取物理信息的策略。

Method: PhysCaP在代码即策略（Code-as-Policy）框架上增加了一个物理信息探索层，包含：(1) 无需训练的物理属性提取模块，利用机器人本体感觉（proprioception）估计物体质量和刚度，无需额外传感器；(2) 双智能体架构：Planner决定何时进行探索以及何时停止，Prioritizer过滤不合理的交互并利用启发式优先级分数对剩余交互排序，从而实现高效、有目标的探索。

Result: 在真实世界桌面操作任务（搜索隐藏物体、检测空罐、寻找成熟牛油果）及LIBERO模拟任务中，被动和朴素交互基线在物理属性隐藏时失败或过度探索，而PhysCaP以更少的交互次数和更短的执行时间达到可比性能。消融实验验证了物理属性提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysCaP证明了物理信息驱动的主动探索对于机器人操作中隐含物理属性感知的有效性，通过选择性交互和物理属性提取，能够在保持性能的同时显著降低探索开销，为操作策略从被动感知走向主动感知提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [34] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V2XBench仿真平台和Chat-V2XBench数据集，并在此基础上构建了AURORA端到端协同驾驶框架，通过双视角感知架构和跨视角查询对齐融合模块，在严重遮挡场景下实现最优驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X基准缺乏闭环评估和语言引导监督，限制了视觉语言模型（VLM）在端到端协同驾驶中的发展。

Method: 提出V2XBench仿真平台（含同步自车-路侧感知和闭环评估）与Chat-V2XBench渐进式VQA数据集；在此基础上提出AURORA框架，采用双视角感知架构，通过CQAF模块在查询级别对齐和融合自车与路侧视角，并由LoRA适配的VLM桥接语义推理与生成式轨迹规划。

Result: 在V2XBench闭环评估中，AURORA在严重遮挡场景下达到Route Completion 98.21%和Driving Score 76.02的SOTA性能，且仅需较低的路侧通信带宽。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了可扩展的V2X-VLM范式，为下一代协同自动驾驶铺平道路。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [35] [TaPeR: Probabilistic Recovery of Sparse Task Precedence Graphs from a Handful of Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21035)
*Adrian Röfer,Karla Stepanova,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从演示中提取任务依赖结构的方法，仅利用简单运动学图和物体相对位姿分布，无需符号谓词标注，即可估计任务步骤间的依赖概率并构建先序图，从而生成多种有效的机器人执行顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务往往只有部分有序的依赖关系（如电池和电路板可任意顺序安装，但必须在闭合外壳前完成）。现有方法需要人工演示中的时序和符号监督来推断任务结构，但符号谓词需要显式grounding，在现实场景中难以获取。因此需要一种不依赖符号监督、仅从演示中提取任务依赖结构的方法。

Method: 方法包含两个主要步骤：(1) 从演示中仅使用简单运动学图和物体相对位姿分布来估计每对任务步骤之间的依赖概率；(2) 利用这些概率估计初始化先序图的边权重，再通过一个过滤管道将该概率图转换为最终的任务依赖图。

Result: 在现有基准数据集和一个包含更长任务、更复杂依赖关系的新数据集上进行了评估。结果表明，该方法相比基线方法能从更少的演示中恢复更准确的任务结构。此外，推断出的依赖图可用于为同一任务生成多种有效的机器人执行顺序。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仅依靠运动学图和物体位姿分布即可有效提取任务依赖结构，避免了符号谓词grounding的困难，并且恢复的依赖图能够支持多种有效的机器人执行顺序生成，展现了灵活的任务重排序能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks are often only partially ordered. For example, when assembling an electronic device, the battery and circuit board may be installed in either order, but both must be in place before the enclosure is closed. Recovering such dependencies enables robots to flexibly reorder subtasks while preserving task validity. Existing approaches typically infer task structure from human demonstrations using both temporal and symbolic supervision. However, symbolic predicates require explicit grounding, which is difficult to obtain in realistic settings. In this work, we present an approach for extracting task dependency structures from demonstrations using only simple kinematic graphs and distributions over relative object poses. From these representations, our method estimates pairwise task-step-dependency probabilities and uses them to initialize the edge weights of a precedence graph. We then introduce a filtering pipeline that converts this graph of probability estimates into the final task dependency graph. We evaluate our approach on an existing benchmark and on a new dataset comprising longer tasks with more complex dependencies. We find that our method recovers more accurate task structures from fewer demonstrations than the baselines. Finally, we demonstrate that the inferred graphs can be used to generate multiple valid robotic execution orders for the same task.

</details>


### [36] [FF-MPCC: High-speed Agile Formation Flight with Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21056)
*Aditya Dandwate,Vit Kratky,Parakh M. Gupta,Martin Saska,Robert Penicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MPCC框架的去中心化编队飞行方法，通过重新参数化和同步机制实现动态编队几何，在高速复杂路径下保持编队完整性，相比时间参数化轨迹跟踪，编队保持性能提升65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机编队飞行中，沿复杂高要求路径（接近平台极限）的敏捷编队飞行仍是一个挑战性问题，特别是需要在满足个体动力学约束的同时保持期望编队形状。

Method: 该方法将编队保持集成到MPCC（模型预测轮廓控制）框架中，提出一种新颖的重新参数化和同步方法用于动态编队几何，并采用去中心化策略确定各无人机的期望位置，使无人机在适应复杂路径进展的同时尊重个体动态约束并保持编队。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化编队飞行方法能够在高速、复杂路径及动态编队形状变化条件下有效协调编队飞行，兼顾编队完整性与路径跟踪效率。

Abstract: Flying in a prescribed formation in an agile manner remains a challenging problem in the field of UAVs, particularly when following highly-demanding trajectories that require flight at platform limits. We address this problem by proposing a novel decentralized approach to formation flight along a given path that integrates formation maintenance into the MPCC framework, allowing UAVs to adapt their progression along complex paths while respecting individual dynamic constraints and maintaining the desired formation. To this end, we introduce a novel reparametrization and synchronization method for dynamic formation geometries together with a decentralized approach to determine the desired positions for the individual UAVs. The proposed approach allows the formation to coordinate high-speed path following without compromising formation integrity. The proposed approach is validated through extensive simulation and real-world experiments involving scenarios with varying complexity of paths and changes of required formation shape on the fly. In comparison to time-parameterized trajectory tracking, we demonstrate improved formation maintenance by 65% in high-speed flight with velocities up to 21 m/s, while achieving comparable times required to reach the goal.

</details>
