Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首次对Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变的角分析，使用LHCb探测器2011至2018年间收集的质子-质子碰撞数据，积分亮度为9 fb^-1。通过拟合双缪子不变质量平方在0.1至12.5 GeV^2/c^4范围内四个区间的角分布投影，确定了轻子前后不对称性A_FB,3/2^ℓ和CP平均角观测量S_1cc。结果与粒子物理标准模型的预测高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对底重子稀有衰变Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-进行角分析，以检验标准模型预测并寻找超出标准模型的新物理信号。这是该衰变道的首次角分析，填补了重子稀有衰变角测量的空白。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在2011-2018年间采集的质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度9 fb^-1），对Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变进行角分布拟合。将双缪子不变质量平方范围0.1-12.5 GeV^2/c^4划分为四个区间，通过拟合角分布的投影来确定轻子前后不对称性A_FB,3/2^ℓ和CP平均角观测量S_1cc。

Result: 测量得到的A_FB,3/2^ℓ和S_1cc角观测量结果与基于粒子物理标准模型的预测值在误差范围内吻合良好。

Conclusion: Λ_b^0 → Λ(1520)μ+μ-衰变的首次角分析结果支持标准模型，未发现偏离标准模型的新物理迹象，为稀有底重子衰变提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: The first angular analysis of ${\it Λ}_{\it b}^{0} \to {\it Λ}(1520){\it μ^{+}μ^{-}}$ decays is presented, using proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector between 2011 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$. The leptonic forward-backward asymmetry, $A_\text{FB, 3/2}^\ell$, and the $CP$-averaged angular observable, $S_{1cc}$, are determined by fitting projections of the angular distributions in four intervals of the square of the dimuon invariant mass between 0.1 and 12.5 GeV$^2/c^4$. The results are in good agreement with predictions based on the Standard Model of particle physics.

</details>


### [92] [New Limits on $n \rightarrow n'$ Transformation from HFIR Cold Neutron Beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21120)
*James M. Rogers,Leah J. Broussard,Christopher B. Crawford,Lisa DeBeer-Schmitt,Matthew J. Frost,Francisco M. Gonzalez,Carolyn O. Haviland,Lawrence Heilbronn,Erik B. Iverson,Yuri Kamyshkov,Mubasshir Khan,Andrew Mullins,David Milstead,Linus B. Persson,Cary Rock,Valentina Santoro,Alexander Saunders,Shaun Vavra,Nathan D. Whittington*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项在橡树岭国家实验室高通量同位素反应堆上利用强冷中子束进行的中子n到惰性中子n'转化搜索实验，未观察到超出背景的信号，将真空中的n→n'转化概率2θ₀²的下限提高了约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假设的中子到惰性中子的转化会违反重子数守恒，并可能揭示暗物质的本质。寻找这一罕见过程对于理解超出标准模型的新物理以及暗物质候选体具有重要意义。

Method: 利用高通量同位素反应堆的强冷中子束，通过超导6.6 T磁体中心放置的薄镉吸收片提供大梯度场，诱发非绝热n→n'转变。实验基于两参数理论模型（质量差Δm和混合真空角θ₀），在磁体下游20米处用³He中子探测器进行信号探测。

Result: 在Δm介于0.1 neV到1000 neV的范围内，未观察到超出背景的信号，从而将真空中n→n'转化概率2θ₀²的下限提高了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该实验为n→n'转化提供了迄今最强的约束，显著排除了这一暗物质解释的可能性窗口，为后续实验指明了更精确的边界。

Abstract: Hypothetical neutron $n$ to sterile neutron $n'$ transformations would violate baryon number $\mathcal{B}$ and point to the nature of Dark Matter. We performed a new search for $n \rightarrow n'$ using an intense cold neutron beam from the High Flux Isotope Reactor at Oak Ridge National Laboratory. We used a theoretical model that describes the transformation $n \rightarrow n'$ with two parameters: a small mass difference $Δ{m}$ between the interaction states $n$ and $n'$ and a mixing vacuum angle $θ_0$. A thin absorbing cadmium wafer was used in the center of the superconducting 6.6 T magnet which provided a large gradient for the non-adiabatic $n \rightarrow n'$ transition. No signal was observed above background in the $^{3}\text{He}$ neutron detector 20 meters downstream of the magnet. This result gives an order of magnitude improvement in the lower limit for the probability $2θ_0^2$ of the $n \rightarrow n'$ transformation in vacuum in the range of $Δ{m}$ between $0.1$ neV and $1000$ neV.

</details>


### [93] [Development of A Novel Compton Camera for MeV Gamma-Ray Measurement in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21216)
*Pingwei Sun,Wenxiang Fang,Guorong He,Zhen Wu,Jinghe Yang,Jiancheng Zeng,Yiyu Pan,Jiacheng Ding,Enzhao Qi,Jiahao Su,Haoran Yang,Bowen Zhu,Mengjiao Xiao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种利用新型闪烁体开发的三层康普顿相机，其能量分辨率优于4%、位置分辨率约2mm，用于填补天文物理中MeV伽马射线"MeV间隙"的观测空白。文章展示了该相机的设计、详细校准和验证结果，并证明其对MeV伽马射线源成像的良好能力，为即将开展的在轨任务做准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeV能段的天体物理伽马射线由于过去几十年探测技术的限制尚未被充分探索，存在著名的伽马射线"MeV间隙"。打开MeV伽马射线窗口不仅对伽马天文学至关重要，也对天体粒子物理的丰富前沿研究（如探测轻暗物质、探测原初黑洞、更深入理解核合成过程）具有关键意义。

Method: 利用新型闪烁体材料，开发了一台三层康普顿相机。该相机通过康普顿散射原理对MeV伽马射线进行成像，实现了优于4%的能量分辨率和约2mm的位置分辨率。文章详细呈现了该相机的设计、校准和验证流程。

Result: 该三层康普顿相机成功实现了优于4%的能量分辨率和约2mm的位置分辨率，并通过详细校准和验证，展示了其对MeV伽马射线源的良好成像能力，满足了即将进行的在轨任务的需求。

Conclusion: 所开发的康普顿相机具备优异的成像性能，能够有效打开MeV伽马射线观测窗口，为未来的天体物理和天体粒子物理研究（包括暗物质探测、原初黑洞研究及核合成理解）提供关键技术支撑，并已为在轨任务做好准备。

Abstract: The astrophysical gamma rays in the MeV energy region have not yet been well-explored due to the limitation of detection technology in the past decades, and the famous gamma-ray "MeV gap" exists. Opening the window of MeV gamma-ray is not only critical for the gamma astronomy but also essential for rich frontier researches in astro-particle physics, such as detecting light dark matter, probing the primordial black hole and better understanding of nucleosynthesis. Using the novel scintillators, a three-layer Compton camera with the energy resolution better than 4% and position resolution of ~2 mm is developed. Here we show the design, detailed calibration and validation results of the novel Compton camera, and demonstrate its good ability of MeV gamma-ray source imaging for the upcoming in-orbit mission.

</details>


### [94] [On-Detector Machine Learning for Beam-Induced Background Rejection at a 10 TeV Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21327)
*Daniel Abadjiev,Eliza Howard,Tsz Ngong You,Ryan Michaud,Benjamin Ryan Roberts,Benjamin Rosser,Karri Folan Di Petrillo,Doug Berry,Arghya Ranjan Das,Jennet Dickinson,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Harshul Gupta,Farah Fahim,Abhijith Gandrakota,Lindsey Gray,James Hirschauer,David Jiang,Shiqi Kuang,Ron Lipton,Mira Littmann,Miaoyuan Liu,Nicholas Manganelli,Petar Maksimovic,Corrinne Mills,Mark S. Neubauer,Aidan Nicholas,Benjamin Parpillon,Jannicke Pearkes,Adam Quinn,Danush Shekar,Ricardo Silvestre,Chinar Syal,Morris Swartz,Nhan Tran,Amit Trivedi,Keith Ulmer,Mohammad Abrar Wadud,Benjamin Weiss*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了在10 TeV缪子对撞机顶点探测器中利用片上机器学习进行束流本底（BIB）抑制的方法，通过像素簇形状区分本底与碰撞产物。三种轻量级神经网络架构在99%信号效率下实现88-90%的数据缩减，硬件资源满足ASIC实现可行性，展示了在像素读出层面直接进行BIB抑制的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的10 TeV缪子对撞机中，缪子衰变产生的束流本底（BIB）会淹没探测器占用率并超出读出带宽限制，亟需有效的本底抑制策略以满足径迹探测器读出要求。

Method: 利用像素簇形状特征区分BIB与碰撞产物，研究三类轻量级神经网络架构，并通过高层次综合（HLS）评估其硬件实现可行性。

Result: 选定的架构在99%信号效率下达到88-90%的数据缩减率，所需硬件资源与潜在的ASIC实现兼容。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在像素读出层面直接进行大量BIB抑制的可行性，为未来缪子对撞机径迹探测器满足读出需求提供了有效策略。

Abstract: A 10 TeV Muon Collider is a compelling candidate for a future energy-frontier facility, offering unprecedented opportunities to explore the fundamental laws of particle physics. Muon decays in the collider ring produce intense beam-induced background (BIB) that can overwhelm detector occupancy and exceed readout bandwidth constraints. We investigate the potential of on-detector Machine Learning for BIB rejection in the vertex detector, exploiting pixel cluster shapes to distinguish background from collision products. We study three classes of lightweight neural-network architectures, and evaluate their implementation feasibility using high-level synthesis. Selected architectures achieve 88 to 90% data reduction at 99% signal efficiency, while requiring hardware resources compatible with potential ASIC implementation. These results demonstrate the potential of performing substantial BIB rejection directly in the pixel readout, providing a strategy for meeting the tracker readout requirements at a future Muon Collider.

</details>

### [95] [Regularized summation of Fourier-Laguerre series in the weighted sup-norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20493)
*S. G. Solodky,Y. A. Volynets*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究半直线上函数从不精确输入信息中的恢复问题，提出了基于拉盖尔多项式的正则化求和方法，分析了其在Wiener类函数上的逼近性质，建立了方法的稳定性条件，并证明了该方法在加权一致范数下的精度和使用的Fourier-Laguerre系数数量上均达到阶最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半直线上函数恢复是数值逼近和信号处理中的核心问题，实际应用中输入信息往往带有噪声或误差，需要构造既对数据扰动稳定又具有最优逼近精度的正则化方法。

Method: 基于拉盖尔多项式的正则化求和方法；在Wiener函数类框架下分析逼近性质；通过建立稳定性条件确保对输入数据小扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了所提出的方法在不精确输入信息下是稳定的，并且在加权sup-范数下的精度以及所使用的Fourier-Laguerre系数数量两个维度上均达到阶最优。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于拉盖尔多项式的正则化求和方法是求解半直线上不精确数据函数恢复问题的有效工具，兼具稳定性与最优逼近效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of recovering functions defined on the half-line from inexact input information. For the proposed regularizing summation methods based on Laguerre polynomials, we analyze their approximation properties on functions from Wiener classes. We establish conditions under which these methods are stable with respect to small perturbations of the input data and order-optimal not only in accuracy in the weighted $\sup$-norm, but also in the number of Fourier-Laguerre coefficients used.

</details>


### [96] [Nyström method for symmetric indefinite matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20531)
*Yijia Chen,Yuji Nakatsukasa,Anjali Narendran,Taejun Park*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Nyström方法在对称不定矩阵上失效的问题（核心矩阵可能严重低估特征值甚至接近奇异），提出了一种通过求解两侧草图最小二乘问题来精心选择替代矩阵M̂的算法，并分析证明了高斯草图需t=O(r)样本、LSS草图需t=O(r log r)样本即可保证残差与最优值相当，LSS方法仅需t²次矩阵元素求值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Nyström方法在对一般对称不定矩阵进行低秩近似时，其核心矩阵A(I,I)可能严重低估矩阵A的特征值，甚至变得（接近）奇异，导致近似效果差或完全失败。现有理论多针对半正定矩阵，因此需要开发适用于不定矩阵的Nyström类算法。

Method: 将Nyström近似中的核心矩阵逆A(I,I)†替换为一个通过求解两侧草图最小二乘问题min_M ||X(A−CMC^T)X^T||_F得到的精心选择的矩阵M̂，其中X是随机草图矩阵。分别研究高斯草图与杠杆分数采样（LSS）两种情形，并理论上分析所需的采样数量与误差保证。

Result: 在高斯草图情形下，t=O(r)个样本即可保证残差||A−CM̂C^T||_*与最优值min_M||A−CMC^T||_*可比；在LSS情形下，t=O(r log r)个样本即可达到理论保证，且LSS方法在确定t个行索引后仅需t²次矩阵元素求值即可计算出M̂（给定C）。通过合成实验和核方法应用验证了结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的两侧草图最小二乘方法有效解决了Nyström方法在对称不定矩阵上的失效问题，提供了可证明的误差保证和实际计算效率优势，为不定矩阵的Nyström近似提供了可靠替代方案。

Abstract: The Nyström method approximates $A\approx A(\,:\,,I)A(I,I)^{\dagger} A(\,:\,,I)^{\top}=CA(I,I)^{\dagger} C^{\top}$, where $C:=A(:,I)\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}$ is a column subset matrix of $A$. When applied to symmetric but indefinite matrices, the Nyström method can fail because the core matrix $A(I,I)$ may severely underestimate the eigenvalues of $A$ and may become (nearly) singular. We address this issue by developing and analyzing an algorithm that carefully chooses $\widehat{M}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}$ in place of $A(I,I)^\dagger$ by solving the two-sided sketched least-squares problem $\min_{M}\|X(A-CMC^{\top})X^{\top}\|_F$, where $X\in\mathbb{R}^{t\times n}$ is a random sketch matrix. We study in detail the cases where $X$ is a Gaussian or a leverage score sampling (LSS) matrix, and show that with oversampling $t>r$ the residual $\|A-C\widehat{M}C^{\top}\|_*$ is comparable to $\min_{M}\|A-CMC^{\top}\|_*$. For the Gaussian sketch, we require $t=\mathcal{O}(r)$ samples; for LSS, we show that $t=\mathcal{O}(r \log r)$ samples suffice for the theoretical guarantee, with the LSS approach carrying the advantage that once a set of $t$ row indices is identified, the approximation requires only $t^{2}$ matrix-entry evaluations to find $\widehat{M}$, given $C$. We illustrate our results with synthetic examples and applications to kernel methods.

</details>


### [97] [A new analysis of the randomly pivoted Cholesky algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20633)
*Ethan N. W. Epperly*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明随机枢轴Cholesky算法能在O(r/ε + r√log r)步内以1+ε因子逼近最优秩r近似，接近理论上限Θ(r/ε)，并给出高概率的迹范数和谱范数误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机枢轴Cholesky算法是计算大规模半正定矩阵低秩近似的主流方法，在实验中表现优异，但其理论分析落后于同类方法，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用数学证明方法，建立了随机枢轴Cholesky算法在期望误差意义下的理论保证，证明其能在O(r/ε + r√log r)步内达到最优秩r近似1+ε倍以内的精度，并推导了高概率下的迹范数与谱范数误差上界。

Result: 证明随机枢轴Cholesky算法期望误差在最优秩r近似的1+ε因子内，所需步数为O(r/ε + r√log r)，几乎匹配任何基于部分Cholesky分解方法的理论最优复杂度Θ(r/ε)；同时给出高概率成立的迹范数和谱范数误差界。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立起随机枢轴Cholesky算法的严格理论保证，使其理论复杂度几乎达到最优，为该算法在实践中的广泛应用提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: The randomly pivoted Cholesky algorithm is one of the leading methods for computing a low-rank approximation to a large positive-semidefinite matrix. However, while it consistently achieves accuracy comparable to or better than competing methods of its type in experiments, its theoretical analysis lags somewhat behind other methods. This paper closes this gap, proving that randomly pivoted Cholesky produces an approximation with expected error within a $1+\varepsilon$ factor of the optimal rank-$r$ approximation in $\mathcal{O}(r/\varepsilon + r\sqrt{\log r})$ steps. This result nearly matches the optimal complexity $Θ(r/\varepsilon)$ for any low-rank approximation method based on a partial Cholesky decomposition (also known as a column Nyström approximation). The paper also presents bounds on the randomly pivoted Cholesky trace and spectral-norm errors that hold with high probability. The mathematical argument is largely due to GPT 5.6-Sol (Pro), with some refinements by the author.

</details>


### [98] [Point Cloud Quality for Meshfree Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20872)
*Mohsen Abdolahzadeh,Oleg Davydov,Isabel Michel,Pratik Suchde*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了现有网格无关点云质量度量指标，并引入若干新指标。通过广泛的数值实验（涵盖椭圆和双曲方程、2D和3D域、不同数值方法参数），评估各质量指标与数值误差的相关性，最终发现六个指标能始终可靠地指示点云质量，同时证明若干常用指标预测精度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格质量已被广泛研究并用于量化优秀网格，但网格无关点云的质量缺乏系统性研究。这一空白使得"生成高质量点云比生成高质量网格更容易"这一常见主张难以得到证实，因此需要系统性地比较和评估现有的点云质量指标。

Method: 作者首先比较现有的点云质量度量指标并提出若干新指标，然后使用网格无关配点法在多种场景（椭圆和双曲方程、2D和3D域、不同数值方法参数）下进行大规模数值测试，最后通过统计各质量指标与数值误差之间的相关性来评估其预测能力。

Result: 实验发现六个质量指标能够始终作为点云质量的可靠指示器，同时表明多种被广泛使用的指标实际上是数值精度较差的预测因子。

Conclusion: 本文填补了点云质量系统研究的空白，为网格无关方法中点云质量的评估提供了可靠指标选择，验证了部分指标的理论价值与实际表现，并为未来点云生成和质量评估研究提供了指导。

Abstract: Mesh quality is very well studied and widely used to quantify a good mesh. In contrast, a systematic study of quality of meshfree point clouds is lacking. This gap makes it difficult to substantiate the common claim that generating a good-quality point cloud is easier than generating a good mesh. Various definitions of point cloud quality have been proposed, some of which have theoretical significance for proving convergence and error bounds, while others are used in computational studies. In this work, we compare and contrast existing point cloud quality metrics and introduce a few new ones. We conduct extensive numerical tests with a meshfree collocation method across a wide range of scenarios, including both elliptic and hyperbolic equations, 2D and 3D domains, and variations in parameters of the numerical method. Based on these tests, we assess which quality metrics best correlate with numerical error. Our findings reveal six metrics that consistently serve as reliable indicators of point cloud quality, while also demonstrating that several widely used metrics are poor predictors of accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [A counterexample to the symmetric-maximizer conjecture for Lyapunov operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20875)
*Daniel Kressner,Bart Vandereycken*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Lyapunov算子Frobenius范数的对称矩阵最优性猜想，构造了一个七阶整数矩阵作为反例，证明斜对称限制范数严格大于对称限制范数，并通过有理分离子和精确算术证书在无浮点计算条件下验证该严格不等式，进而利用直和构造将反例推广到所有n≥7阶，但n=6的情形仍悬而未决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来存在一个猜想：Lyapunov算子（由Frobenius范数诱导）的算子范数总能在对称矩阵处取到。该猜想已验证对于所有不超过五阶的矩阵成立。作者的目标是寻找更高阶的反例以证伪或进一步验证该猜想。

Method: 构造了一个七阶整数矩阵，使得其斜对称限制范数严格大于对称限制范数。为了确保不等式的严格性不被浮点误差干扰，作者使用有理分隔子（rational separator）和精确算术证书（exact-arithmetic certificates）进行验证。此外，通过直和（direct-sum）构造方法，将七阶反例推广到所有n≥7阶的情形。

Result: 成功构造出七阶整数矩阵作为反例，严格证明了斜对称限制范数大于对称限制范数，从而否定了原猜想对七阶矩阵的适用性。直和构造使得所有n≥7阶都存在反例，但六阶情形仍无法确定。

Conclusion: 该猜想对于n≥7的矩阵不成立，反例可通过七阶整数矩阵的直和构造得到；n=6的情形仍然开放，是后续研究的可能方向。

Abstract: It has been conjectured that the operator norm of the Lyapunov operator induced by the Frobenius norm is always attained at a symmetric matrix. The conjecture is known to hold for all matrices of order at most five. We give an integer matrix of order seven for which the skew-symmetric restricted norm is strictly larger than the symmetric restricted norm. A rational separator and exact-arithmetic certificates establish the strict inequality without relying on floating-point computations. A direct-sum construction yields counterexamples in every order $n \geq 7$; the case $n = 6$ remains open.

</details>


### [100] [A Hybridizable Discontinuous Galerkin Method for Wave Propagation in Elastic Beam Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21091)
*Moritz Hauck,Joseph Holten,Axel Målqvist,Andreas Rupp,Lucia Swoboda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对网络上的弹性波传播问题，提出并分析了一种混合可离散化间断伽辽金（HDG）方法，结合能量守恒的隐式时间离散格式，将每时间步的全局问题缩减为仅依赖网络节点数的线性系统，并提出两级重叠加性Schwarz预条件子加速求解，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤网络上的弹性波传播问题数值求解面临两大挑战：一是网络节点的传输条件使得离散后全局系统规模随多项式阶数增长；二是网络中各段纤维长度差异悬殊，导致显式时间格式受严格的CFL条件限制。因此需要一种既能利用网络结构缩减系统规模，又能避免CFL限制的数值方法。

Method: 采用混合可离散化间断伽辽金（HDG）方法进行空间离散，利用网络拓扑结构将全局线性系统缩减为只依赖节点数的规模；时间方向采用能量守恒的隐式时间离散格式以避开CFL限制；针对所产生的大条件数全局系统，引入两级重叠加性Schwarz预条件子，并证明在该预条件下共轭梯度法的均匀收敛性。

Result: 推导了空间和时间上最优阶的先验误差估计；证明了在适定网络假设下预条件共轭梯度法的均匀收敛性；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的HDG方法结合隐式时间离散和两级Schwarz预条件子，能够高效且稳定地求解网络上的弹性波传播问题，兼顾了精度、效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper studies the numerical solution of elastic wave propagation on networks, modeled by elastodynamic equations posed on each edge, coupled at the nodes through suitable transmission conditions. We propose and analyze a hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin method that exploits the network structure to reduce the global problem at each time step to a linear system whose size depends only on the number of network nodes and not on the polynomial degree of the discretization. Combining it with an energy-conservative implicit time discretization, we derive a priori error estimates of optimal order in space and time. The implicit time discretization avoids the severe CFL restriction caused by the large variation in fiber segment lengths. To efficiently solve the resulting, typically ill-conditioned global system, we introduce a two-level overlapping additive Schwarz preconditioner. Under suitable assumptions on the network, we establish uniform convergence of the resulting preconditioned conjugate gradient method. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [101] [Advanced Linear Algebra with Applications - Part I (Numerical linear algebra for PDEs, machine learning, and data assimilation)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21234)
*Victorita Dolean,Jemima Tabeart*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文是高级数值线性代数硕士课程的讲义第一部分，不仅介绍经典算法，还通过现代应用（网络排序、数据同化、大规模数据拟合等）展示数值线性代数较上一代更加核心的地位。讲义覆盖范数、分解、条件数与浮点运算、稀疏矩阵（有限差分/图/机器学习来源）、迭代方法（CG、Lanczos、Arnoldi、GMRES）、预处理、Schwarz区域分解与多重网格等内容，并配有Python代码和习题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值线性代数传统上伴随偏微分方程数值求解而发展，其大稀疏系统主要来源于此。然而如今网络节点排序、气象观测同化、大规模含噪数据拟合等现代应用产生了同样类型的数学问题：规模过大无法直接分解、具有结构、只能通过矩阵-向量乘积访问。作者旨在说明这些看似不同的领域只需极少的核心思想即可统一处理，使该学科的重要性远超上一代。

Method: 讲义按章节逐步展开经典主题，并将每个主题应用到其原始场景之外的新领域：介绍了范数、分解、条件数与浮点运算；覆盖来自有限差分、图和机器学习的稀疏矩阵；讨论平稳迭代及其平滑性质；为共轭梯度与Lanczos方法配套谱聚类与早停正则化；为Arnoldi与GMRES配套PageRank和大规模最小二乘；最终讨论预处理、Schwarz区域分解及多重网格。每章结尾提供习题，并附有可复现数值示例的Python代码。

Result: 讲义揭示了一个核心论点：现代大规模科学计算中最具代表性的问题（网络排序、数据同化、大规模回归）尽管来源各异，但本质上共享相同结构——矩阵过大无法分解、具有特殊结构、仅可通过矩阵-向量乘积访问，从而可用少数的统一数值线性代数思想求解。课程以"学会一个主题，应用于新场景"的方式组织教学，并提供完整的配套代码与历年考题练习。

Conclusion: 本文确立了数值线性代数在现代计算科学中的中心地位，表明其不再只是偏微分方程求解的附属工具，而是处理各类大规模结构化问题的通用数学框架。讲义以实用性和可操作性为导向，为硕士生提供了从理论基础到现代应用的完整学习路径。

Abstract: These lecture notes form the first part of a master's-level course on advanced numerical linear algebra. Their aim is not only to present the classical algorithms, but to show why the subject has become considerably more central than it was a generation ago. Numerical linear algebra grew up alongside the numerical solution of partial differential equations, and for a long time that is where its large sparse systems came from. Ranking the nodes of a network, assimilating observations into a weather forecast, and fitting a model to a large noisy data set now lead to problems of the same kind: too large to factorise, structured, and accessible only through matrix-vector products. Strikingly few ideas are needed for all of them. Each chapter therefore develops a standard topic and then puts it to work outside its original setting. We treat norms, factorisations, conditioning and floating-point arithmetic; sparse matrices arising from finite differences, from graphs and from machine learning; stationary iterations and the smoothing property; the conjugate gradient and Lanczos methods, with spectral clustering and regularisation by early stopping; Arnoldi and GMRES, with PageRank and large least squares; and finally preconditioning, Schwarz domain decomposition and multigrid. We assume a first course in linear algebra. Every section closes with a summary of what should be retained and every chapter with exercises, several drawn from past examinations. Accompanying Python code reproduces the numerical illustrations.

</details>


### [102] [A Harmonic Framework for Vector Fields and Differential Operators on SO(3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21235)
*Ralf Hielscher,Erik Wünsche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于调和级数展开的SO(3)旋转群切向量场与微分算子综合框架，通过使用左右不变标架避免了欧拉角参数化雅可比矩阵带来的数值不稳定性，并在频域中导出了梯度、散度和旋度的显式公式，支持直接对调和系数运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在晶体织构分析软件MTEX中，旋转群SO(3)上的切向量场和微分算子需要稳健且高效的数值实现。传统基于欧拉角参数化雅可比矩阵的切空间基在数值上存在不稳定性，需要寻求更优的数学框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用左不变和右不变标架作为切丛的全局正交标架，替代传统欧拉角参数化的局部切空间基；将切向量场通过其标架分量的调和展开表示，在频域中推导梯度、散度和旋度的显式公式；并证明这些算子保持调和带限性；左/右不变表示之间可在频域直接转换，带宽最多增加1阶。

Result: 成功推导了频域中梯度、散度、旋度的显式运算公式，证明微分算子保持调和带限性，并实现了左右不变表示之间至多增加1阶带宽的频域直接变换。

Conclusion: 该框架为MTEX软件中SO(3)上的切向量场与微分算子提供了坚实的数学基础和数值稳定的实现方案，其谐波设置下展现出的性质使其在频域计算中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive framework for tangent vector fields and differential operators on the rotation group $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ using harmonic series expansions, which provides a mathematical foundation for their implementation in the crystallographic texture analysis software MTEX. The central idea is to employ the standard left- and right-invariant frames as global orthonormal frames of the tangent bundle, thereby avoiding the numerical instabilities of the classical tangent space basis derived from the Jacobian of the Euler angle parametrization. While the choice of frames is primarily motivated by their geometric and numerical properties, their full potential emerges in the harmonic setting. Representing tangent vector fields through harmonic expansions of their frame components, we derive explicit frequency domain formulas for the gradient, divergence, and curl, allowing these operators to be applied directly to the harmonic coefficients. Moreover, we show that these differential operators preserve harmonic band-limitedness. In addition, the left- and right-invariant representations of tangent vector fields can be transformed into one another directly in the frequency domain, with an increase in harmonic bandwidth of at most one degree.

</details>


### [103] [A simple stability analysis of the Lanczos algorithm in finite precision arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21268)
*Tyler Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对不带重正交化的对称Lanczos算法进行了自包含的有限精度分析，推导了扰动的三项递推、Paige正交性损失恒等式、所有计算Ritz值的包含性以及稳定化Ritz值的局部化，并证明了Greenbaum型后向稳定性结果，表明存在一个邻近问题使得精确Lanczos算法产生计算所得的三对角矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Lanczos算法有限精度分析（如Paige和Greenbaum的工作）证明复杂且分散，需要一种更简洁、自包含的分析框架来理解算法在有限精度下的数值行为，包括正交性损失机制和后向稳定性保证。

Method: 采用自包含的有限精度分析方法，推导扰动的三项递推关系，基于此建立Paige损失正交性恒等式，分析Ritz值的包含性与稳定化性质，并构造Greenbaum型后向稳定性证明——展示存在邻近矩阵使得精确Lanczos产生相同的计算三对角矩阵。证明过程通过隐藏迭代次数的多项式因子来简化推导。

Result: 成功推导了扰动的三项递推、Paige正交性损失恒等式、Ritz值包含性定理及稳定化Ritz值局部化定理，并证明了Greenbaum型后向稳定性结果。证明比Paige和Greenbaum的原始证明更为简洁，代价是引入了迭代次数上的多项式因子。

Conclusion: 该工作为对称Lanczos算法的有限精度行为提供了统一的简化证明框架，在牺牲一定紧性（多项式因子）的条件下获得了更简洁、更易理解的分析，加深了对该算法数值稳定性的理论理解。

Abstract: We give a self-contained finite-precision analysis of the symmetric Lanczos algorithm without reorthogonalization. In particular, we derive the perturbed three-term recurrence, Paige's loss-of-orthogonality identity, containment of all computed Ritz values, and localization of stabilized Ritz values. We then prove a Greenbaum-type backward stability result, exhibiting a nearby problem on which exact Lanczos produces the computed tridiagonal matrix. Our proofs simplify those of Paige and Greenbaum, at the cost of hiding polynomial factors in the iteration count.

</details>


### [104] [Basin-Preserving Discretizations of Modern Hopfield Retrieval Dynamics: Energy Cells, Dissipation, and the Attention Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21304)
*Francisco R. Villatoro*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了现代Hopfield网络的检索动力学与注意力更新的时间离散化方法，提出了能量单元（energy cells）概念，证明在逃逸能量以下，有限能量单元同时包含在连续流、松弛注意力映射（0<θ<2）以及隐式欧拉唯一性区间内的吸引域中，并给出参数一致的单位曲率主导项实现无条件耗散与单调插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Hopfield网络的检索动力学是log-sum-exp能量的梯度流，而注意力更新是其精确的差-凸极小化步骤。已有研究关注连续时间动力学与离散注意力更新的对应关系，但尚未系统回答：哪些时间离散化方法既能保持能量衰减和平衡点，又能保持吸引盆（basins of attraction）。这一问题的解决对于理解Transformer注意力机制与Hopfield网络之间的深层联系至关重要。

Method: 引入能量单元概念——包含单一吸引子且不含其他临界点的子水平集的连通分量。主要定理证明：在逃逸能量以下的每个有限能量单元同时包含在三个对象中：连续流的吸引域、松弛注意力映射Ψ_θ=(1-θ)id+θ·attention（0<θ<2）、隐式欧拉方法唯一性区间的吸引域。通过参数一致的单位曲率主项（majorant）证明无条件耗散和离散步骤的单调插值。此外推导了良好分离模式附近的显式局部收缩界（含认证的最优轻微过松弛），刻画了近端隧穿与过冲机制，比较一阶误差常数，建立了标量重参数化的阶数障碍，构造了二阶标量辅助变量方案，并将单元保持推广到Bregman几何中的阻尼差-凸迭代（含非对称有界条件下的认证过松弛窗口）。

Result: 九组数值实验验证了界与失效机制。在二维吸引盆实验中，所有观察到的连续与离散检索之间的分歧都发生在吸引子特定的数值推断逃逸水平之上，验证了理论结果的核心预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为现代Hopfield网络与注意力机制之间的动力学等价性提供了离散化的系统理论框架，确立了松弛注意力映射在0<θ<2范围内对吸引盆的保真度，并推广了差-凸迭代在Bregman几何下的单元保持性质，为理解离散注意力更新的检索可靠性提供了严格的数学保证。

Abstract: The retrieval dynamics of a modern Hopfield network is the gradient flow of a log-sum-exp energy, while the attention update is its exact difference-of-convex minimization step. We study which time discretizations preserve not only energy decay and equilibria but also basins of attraction. We introduce energy cells, connected components of sublevel sets containing one attractor and no other critical point. Our main theorem shows that every finite energy cell below the escape energy is contained simultaneously in the basin of the continuous flow, every relaxed attention map $Ψ_θ=(1-θ)\,\mathrm{id}+θ\,\mathrm{attention}$ for $0<θ<2$, and implicit Euler throughout its uniqueness regime. A parameter-uniform unit-curvature majorant yields unconditional dissipation and a monotone interpolation of each discrete step. We also derive explicit local contraction bounds near well-separated patterns, with a certified optimal slight overrelaxation; characterize proximal tunneling and overshoot beyond the preservation regimes; compare first-order error constants; establish an order barrier for scalar reparametrizations of the relaxed family; construct a second-order scalar-auxiliary-variable scheme; and extend cell preservation to damped difference-of-convex iterations in Bregman geometry, including a certified overrelaxed window under bounded asymmetry. Nine numerical campaigns test the bounds and failure mechanisms. In two-dimensional basin experiments, all observed disagreements between continuous and discrete retrieval occur above the attractor-specific numerically inferred escape level.

</details>
